- **1914.08** `statistical_infection`

- **1914.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1914.10** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-coat-is-just-a-coat` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱起来，走到衣钩下面，让孩子伸手去抓那件旧大衣。粗呢的袖口擦过掌心，Klara先愣住，随后皱着鼻子哭了一声。Josef Bauer笑着把大衣掀开，说“看，只有衣服”，灯影也跟着晃了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +learned_the_coat_rack_

- **1914.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1915.11** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1918.01** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  二楼窗口有人喊了一声，Klara抬头应着，脚却没动。铁环卡在Mitzi Lechner的鞋边，Mitzi Lechner说要再玩一圈。Klara把铁钩递过去，说明天借你。Mitzi Lechner这才挪开脚，用鞋尖把铁环拨到Klara面前。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Emil Weiss在绳子那头喊。Klara踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Emil Weiss数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Klara把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.02999999999999997 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  地窖门虚掩着，Klara下去找腌菜坛，看见Josef Bauer蹲在煤堆后面，把一包用油纸裹好的东西塞进墙缝。Josef Bauer听见脚步，回头时脸上还带着灰；Klara说自己只是来拿坛子，声音比平时高。
  _effects: state_add iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:23; knowledge “大人在酒窖的墙缝里藏了一包用油纸裹着的东西。”; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.02; flag +saw_cellar_bundle; flag +child_secret_active_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger

  Anna Bauer走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Klara把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Klara坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; skill; mood -0.04999999999999999; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/old-school-withholds-papers` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara等到学期过了一半，转学证还没有下来。原来旧学校不肯放人，说Klara欠着图书费。Klara把借的书一本本找齐还回去，管理员却找不到借书卡，最后Klara自己写了一张收据，按了手印。那张收据按上手印后，管理员才从抽屉里找出借书卡，说早就在这里了。
  _effects: cash -1 (跨区转学的居住证明); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1925.06** `r14-edu.entrance-failure::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/short-by-two-points` tags: education, exam_failure, entrance_exam, shame, university, culture, schooling, student
  Klara在放榜墙前从头看到尾，没有找到自己的名字。差两分。Franz Lechner 2把Klara叫到办公室，说作文写得太满，结尾却空了。Elisabeth Winter的名字在第二行，用红笔圈着。Klara回家后把课本码齐，最上面一本压着没写完的志愿表。补考费一克朗，Klara没有去交。
  _effects: cash -1 (放榜墙上的空白); mood -0.14; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.09** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/reciprocated` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Winter把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Klara提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Elisabeth Winter从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1925.11** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/turned-away` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Weiss看了看Klara的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Klara回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +apprenticeship_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1925.11** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-factory` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Josef Bauer把Klara的书包从钉子上取下来，说厂里招装订工，明天就去。Anton Kral傍晚来家里，站在门口劝了半个钟头，Josef Bauer只说灶上还坐着锅。第二天清早，Klara穿着改短的围裙出门，书包留在门后，里面还有半块橡皮。头一个月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_factory; role; education none_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.scholarship-examination::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/lost` tags: education, scholarship_trial, exam, poverty, ambition, university, culture, schooling
  Klara差两分落榜。Franz Lechner 2把卷子摊在讲台上，说作文立意不坏，只是字太挤。回家的路上，Klara把准考证折成四折，塞进鞋垫底下；晚饭时谁也没提考场，只听见勺子碰碗沿。第二天，Klara照常去上课，座位往后挪了一排。
  _effects: mood -0.14; flag +scholarship_lost; skill_

- **1926.02** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/finish-in-falsetto` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Klara，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Klara看见Josef Bauer在第三排低下头，Anton Kral在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Klara用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.02** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +wish:power:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002469_

- **1926.03** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Klara在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Franz Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Klara去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1926.05** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/hid-letter-in-bookshop` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara没有寄信，把信塞进Ferdinand Huber 2常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Katharina Weiss 2说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Klara去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1926.12** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Franz Stein把成绩单按在讲台上，说Klara还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Klara签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.01** `r14-edu.retake-examination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/passed-on-retake` tags: education, exam_failure, retake, persistence, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Klara在同一间教室坐下，桌上的刻痕还是去年的。Franz Lechner 2发卷时说，这次录取线降了两分，但作文要当堂重写。Klara写到一半停下，把去年空着的结尾补上。放榜时名字在末尾，后面跟着一个“备取”的小字。补考费两克朗。
  _effects: cash -2 (第二次走进同一间考场); flag +passed_retake_exam; skill; education secondary_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002184_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Klara分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Klara的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/missing-certificate` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Klara交了钱，Anton Kral却把小学毕业证推回来，说印刷模糊，要他下星期三补一份学校盖章的副本。报名表压在桌角，夜班名额只留到星期五。他连跑两趟学校，第二趟才找到管事的人。
  _effects: cash -5 (报名手续费与押金); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +needs_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1927.05** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sent-to-country` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Klara没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Klara把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Klara说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

- **1927.11** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Klara推刨子。Klara推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Klara，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Klara，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Klara每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/weekly-lesson` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Rosa Stein把老花镜往鼻梁上推了推，让Klara用食指在字盘空位上比画。咖啡馆伙计来续水时，老人把字母表折起来，说下星期带旧字样本来看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; flag +typesetting_mentor; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1928.03** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Kral把一本拉丁文课本推到Klara面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Klara翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Klara把纸抄了一遍，塞进Rosa Lechner 2的历史作业本；那天晚上，Klara第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1928.04** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, university, culture, schooling, student
  Josef Bauer病倒在床，Klara留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Anton Kral托人带来一包旧课本，Klara在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Klara的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:161; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; education apprenticeship_

  Klara趁Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Emil Weiss看。Emil Weiss第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne陪着笑，等门一关，就把Klara的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。
  _effects: relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; reputation 0.03; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 13; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

  Klara又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 13 -> 33; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Klara就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Klara还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Klara在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

  Klara把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Klara把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Josef Bauer醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Klara说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +yf18_unpacked_

  Klara说Otto Berg把答案抄在墨水瓶底，还答应分给自己一半。Franz Lechner 2把Otto Berg的座位调到第一排；第二天，Klara的课桌里多了一张纸条，上面只有一行字：“你以后别走夜路。”Klara把纸条交给Franz Lechner 2，事情从此不再是两个人的事。
  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_named_friend; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003597_

- **1929.07** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/forge-the-permit` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在市政厅的表格上替Josef Bauer签了名，又把日期往前挪了三天。办事员看了Klara一眼，问父亲怎么没来；Klara说他病在床上。印章落下去时，Klara的手心全是汗，出门才发现自己把回执捏成了两半。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:191; cash -2 (stamps and a clerk's fee); flag +yf18_forged_permit; reputation -0.009999999999999995; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1930.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Klara下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Franz Lechner 2从字母表教起，Klara把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Klara第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Klara用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_literacy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002067_

- **1930.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/switched-to-numbers` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, university, culture, schooling, student
  Anton Kral说识字班已经满员，问Klara要不要改上算术课。Klara点头。课上讲的是工钱、尺码和利息，Klara把每一道题都抄在烟盒背面。月底算工钱时，Klara第一次发现工头少记了两个钟头，拿着纸去问，工头把硬币补了回来。算术课学费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); flag +adult_numeracy; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002185_

- **1930.01** `absurd15-wish.crown-for-a-day::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/festival-king-only` tags: wish, power, crown, office, irony, festival, medical, university
  代表团没有带来任命书，只带来一套戏服：市庆游行需要一个“国王”，一天两场，管一顿饭。Klara穿上戏服时，围观的人鼓起掌来。掌声是真的，王冠是假的，晚饭也是真的。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:229; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.039999999999999994_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1930.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state

  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:191; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1930.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/mocked-by-younger-students` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara被年轻同学笑说口音重，念课文时全班哄笑。Klara把书合上，第二天没来。Franz Stein托人带话，Klara只说家里忙，其实每天那个时辰都坐在河边，用树枝在泥地上划自己的名字。河水涨起来的那天，Klara在泥地上写的名字被冲掉了，Klara又写了一遍。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; skill_

- **1930.10** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/home-remedy` tags: health, family, home
  邻居过来看了一眼，教Klara用湿布敷额。Anton Bauer天亮时安静睡了，Klara却耽误半日活计；一克朗五十赫勒的工钱没了，床边的醋味却留了一整天。
  _effects: health 0.05 (家庭护理后烧略退); cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱)_

- **1930.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.work-injury/union-rep` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Anna Moser 2把Klara领到工会代表面前，说护罩少了螺丝不是头一回。代表翻开小本记下机器编号，答应陪Klara去医院，费用由工会垫付，但要Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故单上签字，工头磨蹭半天才落笔。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (工会垫付后自理的零头); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时就医处理伤口); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; flag +union_contact_

- **1931.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/nose-returns` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Klara感冒了三天，鼻子像塞了棉花。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)把十二只小瓶排在柜台上，让Klara闭眼闻；前十一只都答错了，最后一只，Klara说出“佛手柑和一点旧木头”。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)把那只瓶子推到一边，说“记住这个味道，它比你的名字值钱”。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:252; skill; reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-ferdinand-winter; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “第十二只瓶子是佛手柑和旧木头，师傅说这个味道比名字值钱。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Aushilfssetzer @ org-winter-print; role; flag +temporary_journeyman; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

  _effects: cash -1.8 (被扣的字模赔偿); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; mood -0.07999999999999996_

- **1931.07** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1931.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.beer-with-senior` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Klara把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill_

- **1931.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1931.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1932.08** `vienna-life-work-injury/fatal-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, death
  压板落下时Klara没能把手抽出来。工友把人抬到门口，等救护车到时已经没有了呼吸；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne当天下午派人送来三十克朗抚恤金，车间里的机器停了一个钟头，又响起来，像什么都没有发生过。
  _effects: death (crushing injury at the printing press); cash 30 (employer death benefit); role; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1914.03** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/wet-floor` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Alois Lechner端盆出去倒水，灶边淌了一地。Mitzi在摇床里翻身，摇床被湿地板滑得挪了位，撞在桌脚上。Alois Lechner回来时孩子哭得满脸通红，摇床腿松了一条。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (摇床撞上桌脚，受了惊); cash -0.3 (请楼下木匠紧摇床腿（其中0.3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.03_

- **1914.03** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, rural, agriculture
  Mitzi扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Rosa Lechner手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-hedwig-stein; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1918.03** `iw18-school.ink-pot::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-says-nothing` tags: childhood, school, cruelty, shame, teacher, ink, rural, agriculture
  Grete Weiss从讲台抽屉里拿出一块湿布，把桌上的墨迹慢慢擦掉，又把Josef的抄写本翻到新的一页，说“重抄”。整个教室只有布擦过木头的沙沙声；Josef低头写字时，第一次明白有些大人不需要发火也能让人害怕。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; skill; flag +ink_wiped_in_silence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1918.04** `iw18-school.bell-rope::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/falls-into-the-snow` tags: childhood, school, bell, comic, play, injury, rural, agriculture
  煤箱在雪水里滑了一下，Josef整个人摔进墙根的雪堆，铃绳还在头顶晃。Grete Stein赶来时，孩子已经自己坐起来，膝盖破了一层皮，嘴里还咬着半截围巾。那天下午Josef没有上课，被留在火炉边烤湿袜子。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (a fall while reaching for the bell rope); condition; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood -0.030000000000000002; flag +fell_from_bell_rope_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Gustav Berg家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Josef递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Gustav Berg端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Josef把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1919.03** `kernel-child.first-school-day::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-school-fees-keep-home` tags: childhood, school, enrolment, teacher, fees, rural, agriculture, small_town
  学费免了，可校服和课本还要三克朗。Josef Bauer在门口和Grete Weiss说了很久，最后把Josef领回家，说等秋天卖了东西再来。Josef站在校门外，看别的孩子排队进教室，手里的石板一直没有打开。
  _effects: education none; cash -1 (school registration stamp); reputation -0.03; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +kept_from_school_for_fees_

- **1919.03** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-chair-is-for-the-dead` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Josef把盘子摆好，说这把椅子是留给去年埋掉的那个人的。Josef Bauer手里的火柴停了一下，屋里只剩煤在炉膛里塌下去的声响。Josef又说，朋友不喜欢哭，所以今天谁都不许哭；说完自己先红了眼睛。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:27; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +friend_named_for_the_dead; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Josef还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Josef知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Josef把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 65 -> 68; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 60 -> 62; mood 0.02_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Josef后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Josef没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Josef用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1920.10** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Mitzi把皇帝像在Josef眼前晃了晃。Josef数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Mitzi已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Josef把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1920.12** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Josef跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Hermann Kral从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1921.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Josef还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Josef知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Josef把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 68 -> 71; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 62 -> 64; mood 0.02_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Anton Stein在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Anton Stein数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.03 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1921.02** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  碗里倒上醋，兑一半凉水，Josef Bauer把布巾浸透，拧到不滴水，敷在Josef的额头上。布一热就换，换下来的搭在床沿。Josef半夜说梦话，Josef Bauer用手背试了试温度，又去换一盆水。天亮时枕布湿了一片，Josef Bauer的手指白得像泡过石灰。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Josef的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:27; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Maria Berg在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Maria Berg数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店的门铃响一次，Josef就往柜台边挪一步。Otto Stein终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Otto Stein说完就去招呼别的客人。Josef把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Otto Stein看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000282:child_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:craft:pursuing_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Josef在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Josef把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1925.07** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1925.12** `r3-teen.parent-remarriage::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepts` tags: family, remarriage, stepfamily, grief, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner把漏雨的屋檐修好，又在门牌上添了自己的姓。Josef没有叫他父亲，只在他修窗户时递钉子。晚饭桌上，Alois Lechner把最大的一块肉夹给Josef，Josef Bauer低头笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +accepted_parent_remarriage; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Josef分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Josef的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/broken-string` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef换把时用力过猛，弦轴崩了，琴弦抽在手背上。Anton Kral没有责备，只让Josef先学三个月空弦。Josef每天对着镜子拉同一个音，拉到最后，连邻居都不再敲墙，只是经过门口时会放慢脚步。第二天Josef照常来，手上缠着布。弦上还有血。
  _effects: cash -2 (借来的小提琴); skill_

- **1926.07** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/finish-in-falsetto` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Josef，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Josef看见Josef Bauer在第三排低下头，Anton Kral在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Josef用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.009999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1926.07** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.1_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002465_

- **1926.10** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-it-to-the-class` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef以为那是谁开的玩笑，把地图册举起来念给全班听。Franz Lechner 2问是谁写的；教室最后一排有人把书立起来挡住脸。放学后，Elisabeth Bauer在楼梯口等Josef，只说了一句“你念错了一个字”，就从他身边走过去。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:88; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.11** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Josef Bauer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Josef翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Rosa Lechner 2在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Josef翻回来时，Klara把一支烟塞进Josef嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 50; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 20 -> 25; reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 33; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.03_

  Josef交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +workers_youth_quit; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1927.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Josef把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Josef用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 52; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 25 -> 27_

- **1927.06** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/split` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  团里有人偷了市场的香肠，被抓住后供出了所有人。Mitzi把Josef拉到楼梯拐角，让他把责任推给最小的那个，说这样大家都能脱身。Josef没答应，只把口袋里的香肠纸揉成一团。第二天，屋顶上的折叠椅少了一把，Mitzi见了他也不再吹那两声口哨。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +friend_group_split; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation_end friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1927.07** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Moser 2先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Josef每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:117; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1927.11** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpack-and-boil-water` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Josef把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Josef Bauer醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Josef说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000006; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +yf18_unpacked_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/long-shift` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  Josef连续上了十二天夜班，最后一天在机器旁睡着，被工头踢醒。月底多拿了两克朗，咳嗽却一直没好。
  _effects: cash 2 (overtime pay); condition_

- **1928.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Otto Berg一样的白手套；Josef站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 52 -> 44; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  Josef又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。

  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Josef拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Otto Stein找到Josef时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.02999999999999997; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000282:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1936.06** `r15-love.borrowed-room::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/begins` tags: love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef和Mitzi Lechner开始在咖啡馆后门见面。每次只有半个钟头，说的都是不能在家里说的话；Josef回家前会在街角站一会儿，把围巾重新系好，确认身上没有别人的烟味。
  _effects: state_add forbidden-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:425; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +secret_attachment_active_

- **1936.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Otto Stein借给Josef一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Josef睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000282:child; mood -0.06_

- **1936.09** `r1-cul.painting-commission::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-the-demand` tags: culture, painting, patronage, class, desire, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把Helene Kral的手画成搭在椅背上的姿势，Mitzi Stein 2当场撕了定金收据，说这幅画不能挂进客厅。Josef把画布卷起来带走，走到门口时，Helene Kral追出来问：那我呢。Josef说画不卖，人也不卖。
  _effects: cash 3 (肖像上的那只手); mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +refused_patron_demand; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1937.01** `ld18-room.rented-hour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-room-with-two-names` tags: love, room, rent, secret, queer, intimacy, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Gustav Berg 2把顶楼的小房间租给Josef，按钟点收钱，登记簿上要写两个人的关系。Josef说Mitzi Lechner是从林茨来的表妹；Gustav Berg 2看了她们一眼，把“表妹”写得很大，又在旁边添了一句“短住”。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:468; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; cash -2 (the room, the coal and the landlady's discretion（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +rented_room_as_cousins; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Josef没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 30 -> 45; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 25 -> 30; reputation -0.04_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.interview-prep::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/borrowed-collar` tags: work, interview, shop, preparation, class, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Bauer把自己结婚时的硬领借给Josef，又用湿布把领口擦了一遍。镜子只有巴掌大，Josef练了三遍怎么进门、怎么把帽子拿在手里。Mitzi Lechner 3的柜台在两条街外，招的是会写字的人；Josef把地址抄在纸片上，睡前又看了一遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (借来的硬领); flag +work13_prepared_for_interview; skill; relation friend with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1937.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Luise Kral没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1937.06** `state_reaction:forbidden-attachment/betrayal-widens-the-secret` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生以后，Josef发现那间借来的房间不再安全。有人知道钥匙放在哪块砖下面，也知道信该在星期几寄出；Josef没有去质问，只把能烧的纸先烧了。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 10 -> 30; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 5 -> 25; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.grief 0 -> 10; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007493_

- **1937.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-watched-tools` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  河面的雾散尽时，码头上的人走了一半。Josef蹲在石阶上替Rosa Bauer守着麻绳和铁钩，看装卸工在跳板上喊号子。汤送来时还烫嘴，豆子沉在碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Franz Lechner在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Josef排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-charity-tea` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Berta Weiss 2请她们去慈善茶会讲讲“两个女人如何互相扶持”。Josef讲的是房租、煤价和怎么腌白菜；Gustav Berg 2讲的是怎么在冬天不让对方的手裂口。台下有人擦眼睛，也有人一直看表。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.improvisation 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 20 -> 25; cash 1 (a collection at the charity tea); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Josef，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Josef说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Josef把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 33; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 45 -> 41; reputation -0.02_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008005_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/still-hidden` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Josef在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 45 -> 40; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 55 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Josef最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Josef把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Josef说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 33 -> 39; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 41 -> 38; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1938.01** `initiative:found_process:mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61-evt:world-vienna-1912:006314`
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61-evt:world-vienna-1912:006314-1938 as organizer; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61-evt:world-vienna-1912:006314-1938 as organizer; knowledge “圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.04_

  历史进程《圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Josef的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Friedrich Kral拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账); food; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1938.09** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/leave-the-table` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Josef把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Mitzi Stein 2耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Josef走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Josef没有停。第二天上工，Josef的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (一杯咖啡); flag +df18_refused_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “我拒绝了牌桌上的钱，但手指还记得那种感觉。”_

- **1939.01** `ld18-divorce.bench::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/decree-granted` tags: divorce, marriage, scandal, queer, law, public, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Berta Hofer把两封信和一本旅馆登记簿并排放在桌上，问Josef是否承认“长期与他人保持不正当关系”。Josef说承认。法庭里有人咳嗽了一声；Anna Stein的律师要求精神赔偿，Berta Hofer判了离婚，也允许Anna Stein保留婚前的公寓。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:540; reputation -0.08; cash -3 (the lawyer and the settlement（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +divorced_in_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Josef被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Josef低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 39 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  请柬上写着Josef的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Josef怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 30 -> 35; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Josef傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Anton Moser 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Anton Moser 2敲开Josef的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。

  Josef和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Josef在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 52 -> 56; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 49 -> 46; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  星期天的点心拆开纸包，Katharina Weiss 2先问他这半年干了什么。听完没多说，只说明天一早跟他走。料场边那个工头话不多，让他搬了两趟木箱，点了点头。

  Josef请同住的Alois Lechner 2跑一趟，把Katharina Lechner约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Katharina Lechner说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Josef把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。

- **1940.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.library-research/library-found` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  两赫勒从内袋滚到Josef掌心，铜的凉意。他把那册书夹在腋下，和Franz Berg 2一起走下台阶，管理员在锁门前看了眼他的借书条，什么也没多问。
  _effects: skill; cash -0.02 (向管理员缴纳的借书押金); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1940.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1940.12** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Emil Stein在午休时把本子摊在Josef的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Emil Stein当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1940.12** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Katharina Berg把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Katharina Berg低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000253:child; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Josef和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Josef没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 70 -> 85; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 40 -> 45; reputation -0.04000000000000001_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Josef和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Josef在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 56 -> 60; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 52 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Josef是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Josef在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 58 -> 54; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 53 -> 55; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/overpraise` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1941.02** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.cotton-in-ears` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef忍到天亮，第二天去药房买了五分钱的棉花，撕成两小团塞在耳里。夜里的进行曲还剩一点闷响，翻个身也就过去了。此后在楼梯间遇见Hermann Weiss只是点头，再没有进过那家的门。
  _effects: cash -0.05 (在药房买棉花塞耳朵)_

- **1941.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Gustav Hofer把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05000000000000002 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1941.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租)_

- **1941.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Franz Stein在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Josef，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Josef没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 54 -> 62; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 39 -> 43; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Josef听Alois Stein 2报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Josef穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1942.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Josef把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Elisabeth Winter放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Hermann Weiss在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1942.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Josef靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.02999999999999997 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费)_

- **1942.12** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Gustav Berg 2来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1942.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/refuses_grudge` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Berg 2敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Josef那天刚和Gustav Berg 2为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Gustav Berg 2抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Gustav Berg 2低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  Josef只答应让Otto Berg 2住三天，腾出半张床，又托Anton Bauer去打听便宜床位。三天里Otto Berg 2白天出去找活，傍晚回来坐在行军床边上，藤箱始终没有打开。第三天晚上Anton Bauer带回消息，说有一处工人宿舍，一周一克朗；Josef数出一克朗硬币放在箱盖上，替Otto Berg 2垫了第一周。
  _effects: cash -1 (替表亲垫付工人宿舍第一周); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001285:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  Josef把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Anton Hofer 2，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Anton Hofer 2点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; reputation 0.030000000000000027; skill_

  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  Josef没有当场骂Alois Weiss，只让Anna Bauer把顶针先收好，自己蹲下来问是在哪里捡的。Alois Weiss说在楼梯拐角，旁边还有一只装纽扣的铁盒。Josef记得那只铁盒是邻居家的，心里反而更沉。

  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Josef半个月的夜班钱；Josef没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。

  Josef和Gustav Moser 3在厨房桌上把亲戚名单划到只剩两桌，又去当铺问了一只银表能当多少。婚礼那天，借来的桌子拼在院子里，汤里只有几片肉；Gustav Moser 3把戒指戴反了，众人笑了一阵，Josef却没有纠正。
  _effects: cash -10 (婚宴要摆几桌); flag +married_at_a_modest_wedding; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; knowledge “the wedding cost two months of wages”; household_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  一个税务员把Josef的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Josef收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Josef先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。

- **1947.04** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::liberation-anniversary/refused-election-pressure` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, postwar
  解放后的第一场选举，Josef在投票站门口遇见Luise Kral。Luise Kral已经换上西装，问Josef投谁；Josef说“投那个没在占领期开过会的”。Luise Kral笑了笑，把一支烟递过来，Josef没接。
  _effects: flag +refused_partisan_election_pressure; role; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the partisan is backing a candidate”_

- **1947.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Josef把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; flag +union_ledger_public_

- **1947.05** `r14-hist.refugee-arrival::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sent-to-the-transit-camp` tags: refugee_arrival, displacement, housing, postwar, vienna, urban, reform, scarcity
  分配单上盖着“临时安置”的蓝章，卡车把Josef送到城边的旧营房。三层床铺，每层睡两个人，水龙头只有冷水。Berta Weiss 2说最多住六周，但墙上的登记表已经换过四次。Josef把箱子塞在床底，夜里听见邻铺的人在数家里的名字，数到第五个就停了。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (火车站分配来的门牌); flag +transit_camp; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; move place-wien_

- **1913.03** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-enough` tags: infancy, food, household
  Rosa Lechner在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg给Rosa喂奶，随后把洗净的布尿片挂在窗边。孩子吃饱后睡着了，锅里还留着半碗燕麦糊。
  _effects: cash -2 (milk and oatmeal)_

- **1913.10** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-shadow-bows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, rural, agriculture
  煤气灯芯拧到最小，天花板上一件挂着的大衣被风推得晃了晃。Rosa躺在摇篮里忽然笑出声，朝那片影子伸出两只手。Rosa Lechner抬头看了很久，只看见衣钩和一块掉漆的墙皮。
  _effects: mood 0.02; flag +laughed_at_ceiling_shadow_

- **1914.05** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.10** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Rosa在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Franz Lechner在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

- **1913.06** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Josef Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1915.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Anna Moser正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Emil往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。

- **1917.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death

  _effects: death (暴力)_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Lena的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Lena在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1918.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Lena踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josefine Hofer把Lena抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Lena举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05_

  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  “你看看这角。”Emil Weiss把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Lena伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Emil Weiss从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Lena手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Lena一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/folds-notices` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一捆教区通告交给Josefine Hofer带回家。Lena放学后在厨房桌上折页到夜里，手指沾满油墨，两周后交回六千张，拿到三克朗二十赫勒；钱交给Josefine Hofer时，Lena的指尖已经洗不干净，指甲缝里留着一圈黑；家里从此多了一个能挣几个铜板的孩子。
  _effects: cash 3.2 (folding parish notices); condition; role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Lena站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Alois Stein在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Lena掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Lena才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1921.07** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  “Notwendigkeit”少了一个字母，Franz Lechner 2没说话，只敲了敲桌角。Lena回家时手指上全是墨水，母亲问起，只说写了点作业。
  _effects: mood -0.059999999999999984; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1921.09** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/fought-in-the-snow` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  Lena在放学路上把教师儿子推倒在雪里，两个人滚成一团。Grete Stein赶来时，先扶起自己的儿子，再问Lena为什么打人。Lena说她先切我的橡皮。Grete Stein说一块橡皮也值得动手？那天Lena在走廊站到天黑。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +fought_teachers_son; relation rival with person-grete-stein_

- **1921.10** `p9-childhood-school.truant-prater/truant-caught-by-patrol` tags: childhood, school, teacher, peers, lessons, truancy
  桥头的风把河腥味吹上来，戴毡帽的Gustav Berg伸手一拦，问Lena和Elisabeth Bauer是哪所学校的。Elisabeth Bauer转身就钻进来往的人堆，Lena愣了一拍，已经被抓住书包带。Gustav Berg翻开小本子记下名字，又问住哪条街，说这两天会有人上门。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Josef Bauer朝Lena招手。Lena挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Lena把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1922.06** `r1-child.punishment-peas::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/left-hand-for-the-ruler` tags: school, punishment, teacher, dialect, authority, university, culture, schooling
  Anton Stein举起戒尺时，Lena先把左手伸出去，说打右手明天写不了字。教室里有人笑，Anton Stein愣了一下，尺子落在左手心上，只打了两下。那天Lena用左手抄完了五十遍，字歪得像刚学走路的笔画。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +offered_left_hand_to_ruler; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-list-instead` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Franz Lechner 2把教案夹到腋下，走廊尽头有人晃着钥匙串催。Franz Lechner 2从本子背面撕下一页，匆匆写了三道题，墨迹还没干就递过来。Lena把纸对折两次，塞进衣兜，站在校门口看Franz Lechner 2走远，纸角在手心里越捏越软。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +boarding_school_arrival; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  _effects: mood -0.18; reputation -0.1; flag +letter_read_aloud; relation rival with person-gustav-berg_

  Lena的肚子在办公室里叫了一声。Franz Lechner 2问早饭吃了什么，Lena说喝了水。Franz Lechner 2从抽屉里拿出半块面包，让Lena吃完再讲题。题讲完，面包还剩一口，Lena用纸包好，带回去给妹妹。妹妹问面包是哪来的，Lena说是老师给的，妹妹不信，还是吃完了。
  _effects: food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Klara点了牛奶咖啡，Lena要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡（其中0.12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Lena把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.12** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.delay` tags: home, market, food, social
  三个克朗拖了九天。玛丽亚收下时数都没数，只把Lena的名字从木牌上抹去，粉笔灰落在卷心菜上。Anna Moser拎着篮子从旁边过去，没停脚。
  _effects: cash -3 (延迟偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; reputation -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

  火车在清晨进站，Emil Weiss把Lena的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Lena把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。

- **1931.07** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Anton Hofer 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Lena把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Anton Hofer 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1931.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Franz Huber借给Lena一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Lena睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

  有人把一封刚从故乡寄来的信读出声，读到“他在维也纳大概忘了家里”时，桌边的人都笑了。Lena也跟着笑，手却停在汤勺上；他想起自己上个月没有寄出的那张汇款单。

  Josefine Hofer把账本合上，指着店里的空位说那个位置比讲堂稳当。Lena一直说到汤凉透，父亲没有再开口，母亲收拾碗碟时故意弄出响声。当晚他把听课证放进抽屉最底层。
  _effects: relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +study_deferred_by_family_

  他们终于摸到公证处的后窗，撬开的抽屉里却没有遗嘱，只有一叠慈善捐款收据，数目和报纸上公布的对不上。Lena把收据塞进外套，找到一位律师，换回七克朗；律师说，这不是盗窃，是取证。Otto Weiss问那遗嘱怎么办，Lena说，先让死人再等一等。
  _effects: cash 7 (把慈善收据交给律师的酬劳); knowledge “公证处抽屉里的慈善捐款收据与报纸公布的数目对不上。”; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; condition; flag +stole_charity_receipts; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1932.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena上楼敲了Anna Stein的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Anna Stein把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Lena能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Lena和Anna Bauer把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.010000000000000009; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, reform, medical
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Anna Stein忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Lena陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Anna Stein站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1933.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  疗养院的护士接过行李，把床号写在小黑板上。Lena躺下时听见走廊尽头有人在放唱片，咳嗽没那么紧了。安娜的信第三天到，说花浇过了，房东没来催租。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

  Lena从一九〇四年的画报上抄下一段描写雨夜的话，把地名和人名换掉，寄给一家征文比赛。抄的时候，Lena告诉自己只借这一小段；寄出去之后，却在邮局门口站了很久，反复回想那段话里有没有一个词是别人一眼就能认出来的。
  _effects: state_add fa17-plagiarist state:world-vienna-1912:321; skill; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +borrowed_paragraph_

  Lena在同学会上见到Rosa Bauer，两人都老了不少。Rosa Bauer说还记得那间靠窗的床，冬天玻璃上结冰，Lena用指甲在上面画过一只鸟。Lena笑起来，说早忘了。散场时，Rosa Bauer把电话号码写在纸巾上，Lena放进了口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +boarding_reunion; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1934.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Lena把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Lena读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; household; role; role; role; cash -8 (apron, shoes and wedding clothes)_

  Lena和Marie Huber一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Lena去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Lena先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1939.07** `r2-faith.refugee-clothing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-away-for-papers` tags: charity, refugee, clothing, aid, queues, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton Huber 2把Otto Weiss的临时证翻过来，说签发日期过了三天，不能领。Otto Weiss说火车站的人把日期写错了，Anton Huber 2说那就去找火车站。Lena把自己的围巾解下来递过去，Otto Weiss没有接，转身走进雪里。

  Lena说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Gustav Weiss 2一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。

  Lena说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Emil Weiss把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Emil Weiss在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。

  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1940.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Emil Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Emil Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation

- **1914.02** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Josefine Hofer把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Anton吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1914.07** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Hofer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food

  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  Anton放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Anton的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Anton也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。

  Anton踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Anton Kral让Anton把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Anton把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Anton把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1923.02** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Josefine Hofer的手举起来，又慢慢垂下去。皮带被扔回门后，Anton被留在桌边，把碎瓷片包进旧报纸里。窗外有人在巷子里追跑，鞋底拍在石板上。Anton听着，手下一滑，瓷片在食指上割出一条细线，血珠慢慢冒出来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1923.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/traded-for-picture` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  报亭后面的墙根下，Anton把咬了一半的糖递过去，换回一张火车头画片。糖纸被风吹到摊主的脚边。Josef Bauer弯腰捡起来，把画片从Anton手里抽走，说先把地扫了。Anton扫到墙根，Josef Bauer才把画片还回来。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1923.07** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Anton的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Anton追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Lena从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1923.07** `r1-child.epidemic-closure::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/pasteboard-boxes-during-closure` tags: school, epidemic, closure, labour, quarantine, medical, university, knowledge
  停课第三天，Josefine Hofer让Anton去印刷所领糊纸盒的活。纸盒堆在床边，Anton糊到半夜，手指被浆糊粘得张不开。Gustav Berg来给邻居孩子看病，看见Anton在灯下数纸盒，说这孩子该睡觉。
  _effects: cash 2 (停课十四天); condition; flag +child_labour_during_closure_

- **1924.01** `kernel-edu.appeal-exam-result::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-appeal-retake` tags: task, adolescence, school, appeal, official, university, culture, schooling
  Gustav Berg同意九月给一次补考，却不保留名额。Franz Lechner 2每天放学后留Anton补算术，用旧考卷和一支短铅笔。家里用六克朗买了参考书。Anton把补考日期写在手心，回家路上一直攥着。
  _effects: cash -6 (reference books for the retake（其中6克朗记入债务）); skill; flag +retake_granted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1924.05** `r3-teen.first-cigarette::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/coughs` tags: smoking, friendship, risk, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Rosa Bauer把烟卷塞进Anton嘴里，划了根火柴。第一口呛得Anton眼泪直流，第二口装作没事。Rosa Bauer笑得蹲在地上，说城里人都这么学。Anton把烟夹在指间，直到烧到指节才扔掉。
  _effects: flag +tried_first_cigarette; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1924.07** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/excluded` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, university, culture, schooling
  Rosa Bauer说入伙要交二十个铜板买蜡烛，Anton掏遍口袋只有九个。他们让他在楼梯口等，等到天黑也没人下来。楼上传来笑声，像隔着一层厚被子。Anton把九个铜板在楼梯上排成一排，又一个个收进口袋。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +excluded_from_friend_group; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1924.10** `vienna-youth-threshold.school-leaving/print-shop-trial` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anton Kral把离校证明折好塞进Anton手里，说西梅林区那家印刷厂正缺跑腿。Anton当天下午就沿墙根走到厂门口，油墨和湿纸的味道从门缝里钻出来。工头记下名字，让下周一再来试工，先不算正式雇用，也不给工钱。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/teacher-explains` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Anton在办公室门口站到下课，才敢把作业本递进去。Anton Kral翻到错得最多的那一页，没有骂，只让Anton把题目再读一遍。读到第三遍，Anton忽然明白自己一直看漏了条件。Anton Kral把红笔放下，说以后不懂就来。那天以后，Anton的作业本上多了几行红字，都是Anton Kral夜里改的。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1925.02** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Josefine Hofer病倒在床，Anton留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Anna Bauer托人带来一包旧课本，Anton在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Anton的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1925.02** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Anton推刨子。Anton推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Anton，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josefine Hofer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1925.09** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Anton追下车，把那只手套递还给Lena。Lena说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Anton回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:65; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Hedwig骂了一句，Lena却把围巾解下来递给Anton；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 20; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Lena。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 48 -> 53; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1926.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/no-vacancy` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Anton问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”_

  第二天，Anton在教室里把滑道说成三层楼高、底下是鳄鱼池。Berta Kral配合着补充细节，连Emil Berg的灯都被说成探照灯；全班听得入神，只有后排的Hedwig问了一句“那你为什么裤子破了”。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +yf18_prater_story; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1926.11** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-friend-library` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, university, culture, schooling
  Anton在图书馆门口认识Klara，两人合用一张借书证，轮流把书带回家。Klara读得快，常在书页边留铅笔字；Anton读到那些字，像在跟人说话。冬天过去，书证上多了两个名字。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; skill; flag +study_friend; mood 0.03999999999999998_

  Anton的成绩打动了本地学校，拿到一份覆盖学费的奖学金；家里第一次不用为这笔钱吵到半夜。
  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:86_

- **1927.01** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-moves-away` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi的父亲在车站找到活，全家要搬去另一区。临走前，Mitzi把一本旧书塞给Anton，扉页上写着地址。Anton把书读完，按地址写了信，信被退回来，上面盖着“查无此人”。那本旧书Anton一直留在书架上。
  _effects: knowledge “朋友搬走前留下的地址已经找不到人了。”; skill; flag +friend_moved_away; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:65; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, university, culture, schooling
  Anton把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Anton在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Anton把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1927.02** `r1-child.cuff-answers::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-examination-board/oral-examination` tags: exam, cheating, school, shame, numeracy, reform, medical, schooling
  考试委员会改用口试，Anna Bauer坐在长桌后面，面前摆着一排编号的木牌。Anton抽到第七号，题目是“说出三个分数的加法”。他站在屋子中央，听见自己的声音在墙上弹回来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +oral_exam_introduced; skill_

- **1927.07** `yf18-family.one-apprenticeship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/yield-to-the-younger` tags: siblings, apprenticeship, scarcity, family_duty, choice, reform, medical, schooling
  木匠铺只收一个学徒，押金却要两份。Josefine Hofer把两张表格摊在桌上，说家里只拿得出一次；Anton看见Lena把铅笔削得很尖，最后说让Lena去。第二天，Anton去码头找了半日工，回家时Lena正在院子里练刨花，刨子是新领的，柄上还缠着纸条。
  _effects: state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:112; cash 2 (half-day wages from the docks); relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_yielded_apprenticeship; mood -0.020000000000000018; education apprenticeship; role_

  Emil Berg把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Anton看期限、工钱和出师考试。Anton签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:113; education apprenticeship_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Anton下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。

  _effects: cash -1.5 (没拿到最后一个月的整月工钱（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.08000000000000002; unemployment (不告而别离开作坊); relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter_

  _effects: cash -10 (青年会远足车费（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  床架斜靠在垃圾桶上，床垫用绳子捆着，Anton蹲下把散在石板上的勺子一只只捡回布袋。Luise Kral的过道只放得下箱子，床架只好留在院里。收容所门口发毯子的人说只剩两张，孩子裹着毯子先睡了。
  _effects: residence; cash -2 (收容所登记与寄存箱子的杂费); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood -0.07999999999999996_

- **1931.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.11** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Anton托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Anton晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Anton却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.07999999999999996; mood -0.08000000000000002; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

  _effects: reputation -0.03; flag +public_health_notice_torn_down; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the market crowd blames the notice for frightening customers”_

  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中6.6克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

  Anton用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: health -0.03 (鞋底不平磨出水泡); skill_

  Anna Moser把收据本合上，说这条街上等着租的人排到了巷口。Anton把准备好的账目念到一半，Anna Moser已经转身去敲下一家的门。第二天，门缝里塞进来一张纸条，欠租的数目后面多了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (房东的收租日（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +rent_increase_refused; relation rival with person-anna-moser_

  Anton把外套的肩线拆开，往里收了四指，袖口剪掉一截，改出一件到胯骨的短上衣。剩下的布头拼成两只手套，针脚粗，戴着能挡风。她在镜子前转了一圈，肩还是有点空，但总算能穿出门。

  _effects: cash -1 (雨里的护照队（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +missed_passport_window; knowledge “passport numbers are exhausted before noon”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +ration_queue_closed; knowledge “the district quota can run out before the window closes”_

  _effects: relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Anton把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Emil Weiss站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Anton手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。

  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “卫生巡查员把下水道堵塞记进了记录，说会通知房东限期修”_

  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为圣马克斯殡葬合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。

  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债); health -0.00499999999999993 (母亲的药钱被挪去还债，家里几夜没睡好); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -0.06 (教堂捐献与烤土豆); food; mood 0.050000000000000044_
