### 159 subject events

- **1913.01** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Hofer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1913.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1913.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1917.06** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Hedwig立刻回家。铁环还在Ferdinand Huber脚边，Hedwig蹲下去抽，抽不动。Ferdinand Huber说除非明天把铁钩带来。Hedwig点头，Ferdinand Huber把脚一抬，铁环滚到Hedwig手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; mood -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Hedwig前面的大婶一直往后靠，Hedwig就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Hedwig的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/half-day-place` tags: childhood, school, fees
  学校只收半日班，Grete Weiss说下午还有空位，但课本要家里自己买。Josefine Hofer先交了一克朗，把Hedwig的名字写在候补名单上。
  _effects: education primary; cash -1 (partial school fee); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  “老师，我能留下来吗？”Hedwig问完就盯着自己的鞋尖。Grete Stein想了一会儿，说早上来，人少。天没亮，Hedwig已经站在校门口，手指冻得伸不直。教室里只有炉火和粉笔灰的味道，Grete Stein把一道竖式拆成三行，让Hedwig跟着念一遍。
  _effects: skill; health -0.020000000000000018 (天没亮出门，早春的冷); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1920.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Grete Stein让Hedwig站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Grete Stein在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Hedwig把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary; health -0.010000000000000009 (cold classroom)_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Anna Stein把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Hedwig伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Anna Stein从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Hedwig手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Hedwig一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1920.07** `r1-child.epidemic-closure::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/temperature-line-in-soot` tags: school, epidemic, closure, labour, quarantine, medical, university, knowledge
  黑板旁贴了张告示：猩红热，停课两周。Grete Weiss让每个孩子带一张纸回家，上面抄着“每日体温”。Hedwig不会写“体温”，Josefine Hofer用灶灰在桌上画了一道线，说高过这条就说话。
  _effects: condition; flag +school_closed_for_scarlet_fever; skill; education primary_

- **1920.07** `vienna-school-childhood.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.extra` tags: school, social, childhood
  Hedwig放学后留在学校汤厨房帮Grete Stein搅锅，木勺比Hedwig的手臂还长。Grete Stein多给了Hedwig一勺扁豆汤，Hedwig用铁皮饭盒带回家，倒进母亲的锅里。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; skill_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Hedwig放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Hedwig的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Hedwig也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Hedwig把错题纸举到讲台边，话说到一半就低下去了。Franz Lechner 2放下红笔，说“明早提前来”。第二天Hedwig摸黑走到Leopoldstädter Volksschule，教室门已经开了，炉子冒着青烟。Franz Lechner 2把竖式写在黑板上，先擦掉一半，让Hedwig补上，再一笔一笔讲为什么。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1921.04** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/fought-in-the-snow` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig在放学路上把教师儿子推倒在雪里，两个人滚成一团。Franz Lechner 2赶来时，先扶起自己的儿子，再问Hedwig为什么打人。Hedwig说她先切我的橡皮。Franz Lechner 2说一块橡皮也值得动手？那天Hedwig在走廊站到天黑。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +fought_teachers_son; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1921.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/found-coin-under-rack` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  明信片架子底下有灰，Hedwig的手指碰到一枚硬东西，摸出来是两赫勒。Gustav Berg背对着柜台数报纸，Hedwig把硬币攥在掌心，指给Gustav Berg看一张风景画片。找零时Gustav Berg的手停了一下，多看了一眼，还是把画片递了过来。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (买画片花掉捡到的硬币); condition; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Rosa Lechner把Hedwig拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Hedwig把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Hedwig蹲下把两包并排，按Rosa Lechner说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1922.04** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.win` tags: school, social, childhood
  拼写比赛结束时，Anton Kral敲了敲讲台说“记住这个词”。Hedwig袖口上多了一枚小十字章，回家路上Luise Berg追着要看，最后用三颗李子换了眼福。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1922.11** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Hedwig却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Mitzi从人群里挤过来，先看了看Hedwig的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Mitzi把Hedwig拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1923.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Franz Lechner 2让Hedwig把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Hedwig把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Hedwig把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1924.01** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/admonished-and-sent-home` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Gustav Berg把案卷合上，问Hedwig知不知道错了。Hedwig的腿在长椅上晃着，够不着地，说“知道”。Josefine Hofer在旁边一直点头，Luise Kral把一张写着“训诫”的纸递给她。出门时，Josefine Hofer把纸折好放进内衣口袋，怕人看见。
  _effects: flag +admonished_by_juvenile_court; role; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the juvenile court keeps a record even without a sentence”_

- **1924.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/first-recital` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, university, schooling, school
  Hedwig在期末演奏会上拉完一首短曲，弓毛断了两根，最后一个音还是稳住了。Franz Lechner 2在台下点头，师母把手套摘下来鼓掌。回家后，Hedwig把琴擦了三遍，才发现肩托把脖子磨出一道红印，碰一下就疼。Hedwig没有告诉任何人。琴盒没关。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +first_recital; skill_

- **1925.01** `major-education-scholarship-examination/half-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, adolescence, major
  成绩排在第七名，离全免只差两个名次。Franz Lechner 2替Hedwig去问，回来把一张纸摊在桌上：学费减半，另给十五克朗书费，车钱和冬衣自理。Josefine Hofer算了一夜，把准备换炉子的钱挪出来，第二天在同意书末尾按了手印。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 15 (half scholarship book grant); role; flag +half_fee_place; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1925.01** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把叠成方块的纸条塞给Mitzi，第二天在课桌里找回同一张纸，边角压平了，字没有被打开过。放学时Mitzi从另一边走了，Hedwig坐在电车上，一直坐到终点站。
  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; flag +first_love_rejected; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.teacher-recommendation/night-school-first` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:education
  “先念夜校。”Franz Lechner 2用铅笔敲了敲练习本的封面，说簿记和德语拿到证书，Franz Lechner 2才肯写推荐。Hedwig问清一学期要一克朗，又问了上课的钟点，回家路上绕到六区，隔着玻璃看教室里的黑板。第二天Hedwig把铜币数好，报名时还问了几句考试怎么算。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; cash -1 (夜校一学期报名费和课本钱); flag +enrolled_night_school; skill_

- **1925.02** `r14-edu.entrance-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/apprenticed-instead` tags: education, exam_failure, entrance_exam, shame, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Josefine Hofer在饭桌上说，既然考不上，就去印刷所当学徒，至少能挣口饭。Hedwig第二天跟着Josefine Hofer去见工头，围裙比身子大一圈。机器响起来时，Hedwig把师傅说的铅字字号记在手心，晚上洗手时全花了。第一个月工钱两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (放榜墙上的空白); flag +apprenticed_after_failure; role; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1925.03** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Hedwig看期限、工钱和出师考试。Hedwig签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-franz-lechner; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:66; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, university, culture, schooling
  Franz Lechner把一条绷带绕在Hedwig的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Hedwig在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Franz Lechner发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”_

- **1926.07** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Hedwig和Lena从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Mitzi Weiss 2提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1926.10** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/evening-only` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, reform, medical, schooling, school, teacher
  学校没有多余名额，只给Hedwig留了夜校的位置。白天在工厂站一天，晚上再去教室坐三个钟头；老师说先试试，撑不住也不算丢人。
  _effects: state_add night-school state:world-vienna-1912:87; role_

- **1926.10** `r3-teen.first-cigarette::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/coughs` tags: smoking, friendship, risk, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Winter把烟卷塞进Hedwig嘴里，划了根火柴。第一口呛得Hedwig眼泪直流，第二口装作没事。Elisabeth Winter笑得蹲在地上，说城里人都这么学。Hedwig把烟夹在指间，直到烧到指节才扔掉。
  _effects: flag +tried_first_cigarette; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1926.12** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/tested-the-new-brake` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Franz Lechner拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Hedwig问这是不是坏了，Franz Lechner说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Marie Huber在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999985; condition; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:night-school/annual/held` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又撑过一年夜校；工位和课桌仍然互相偷时间，但进度在缓慢累积。
  _effects: state_parameter night-school.progress 0 -> 15_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.teacher-recommendation/ordinary-certificate` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:education
  “我只能照实写。”Franz Lechner 2把信推过来，纸上只有三行字，印章盖得端端正正。Hedwig看完，把信举到窗口又看了一遍，没找到“印刷”两个字。Franz Lechner 2说，要是想让Franz Lechner 2写推荐，先把夜校的簿记念完。Hedwig把信收好，没再争。
  _effects: flag +has_reference_letter; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1927.06** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Hedwig穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Anton从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Hedwig走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:116; health 0.019999999999999997 (cold river water and a long swim); mood 0.03_

- **1927.07** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Bauer看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Josefine Hofer求了半天，Alois Bauer答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Lena干了三天就走了，Hedwig留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Alois Bauer才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1927.12** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Hedwig追下车，把那只手套递还给Rosa Lechner 2。Rosa Lechner 2说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Hedwig回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。

- **1927.12** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Hedwig把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josefine Hofer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:115; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer_

  厂里不肯批假，Hedwig只好辞工。疗养院的账单先欠着，回到维也纳时，原来的位置已经有人；Hedwig把疗养院的收据和工票放在同一个铁盒里。铁盒盖上之前，他又看了一眼工票上的编号。
  _effects: cash -20 (sanatorium bill and travel（其中19克朗记入债务）); move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; health 0.019999999999999997 (sanatorium rest improved the cough); condition; role; knowledge “厂里不批疗养假，辞工才能进疗养院；回来时位置已经有人，收据和工票只能放一起。”_

- **1932.04** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  屋里只有一张床，Hedwig把椅子拼在床边守了三夜，给Josefine Hofer敷药换布。请假扣的工钱让人心疼，但腿伤见好；第四天早上，Josefine Hofer自己端起了粥碗。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1932.06** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-part-cash-part-furniture` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1932.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/advance-with-room-claim` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig把十四克朗的整张票子压在药费单上，说这钱不用还，只要求母亲那间朝院子的屋子以后归自己照看，钥匙也归自己管。Anton的咖啡杯在桌上顿了一下，说照看可以，房契上的名字另说；药费单总算从糖罐底下抽了出来，兄妹之间却多了一层账。
  _effects: cash -14 (先垫付母亲全部药费（其中14克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +claims_mother_room_

- **1933.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, rural
  Franz Stein坐在床边，Hedwig用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Franz Stein闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 61 -> 66; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 3 -> 0; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +hairwash_sunday_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Hedwig最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Hedwig把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Hedwig说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 0 -> 6; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 45 -> 55; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1933.01** `major-task.scholarship-application/application-refused` tags: task, task:education, education, scholarship, school
  办事员翻到最后一页，说今年优先收有人担保的申请。Franz Stein陪着Hedwig又去了一趟，得到的回答仍是“名额已满”。走廊里还坐着两个同样被退回来的学生，其中一个已经把书卖掉了。回家的路上，Hedwig把申请表折成四折；两克朗报名费和一张印花税票没有退。
  _effects: cash -2 (application fee and stamp duty); flag +scholarship_refused; knowledge “助学金名额常留给有担保人的申请者。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1933.05** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-statement-is-taken` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, rural, agriculture
  Emil Weiss把Hedwig叫进办公室，桌上摊着Franz Lechner 2交来的三页证词。Hedwig逐条回答，Emil Weiss逐条记录，写到“是否曾诅咒他人”时停了很久。最后Hedwig被允许回家，但门口多了一个每月来一次的访客。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 20 -> 45; state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 2 -> 12; condition; reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1933.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Hedwig就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Josefine Hofer摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1933.12** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  Hedwig捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Anna Moser点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Hedwig和Elisabeth Bauer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Elisabeth Bauer把煤钱推过来，Hedwig接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Hedwig被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Hedwig低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 6 -> 16; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005870_

- **1934.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, rural, agriculture
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Hedwig最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Hedwig把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Hedwig说和平时一样。

- **1934.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 12 -> 4; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.seek-apprenticeship/p10-edu-work-tasks.seek-apprenticeship.deposit` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, craft
  他把旧刨子放在工作台上，刃口的缺口在灯下看得清。Alois Lechner用拇指试了试，说先用一个月。那天起他收工后留下来磨刃，磨石上的水混着铁屑，指尖冻得发木。
  _effects: cash -2 (买磨刀石与补工具柄（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); flag +apprenticeship_trial_

- **1935.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/tells-the-priest` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:391; reputation -0.01999999999999999; condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Hedwig和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Hedwig在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 66 -> 70; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 22 -> 19; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:251_

- **1935.01** `kernel-civic.petition-drive::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/civic-petition-refused` tags: politics, petition, reform, neighbor, organization, rural, agriculture, small_town
  她把签名纸送到市政厅，Franz Lechner说格式不对，要按街道分开。她回去重抄，发现有几个名字已经认不出；再去时，窗口换了一个人，说前一班没有交接。她坐在市政厅台阶上重抄，风把纸吹到花坛里。
  _effects: flag +petition_refused; reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; cash -0.5 (重抄签名纸的纸笔钱); knowledge “请愿书要按街道分页，窗口换班时前一班可能没有交接”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1935.02** `r2-rural.farm-servant::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-for-the-year` tags: rural, labour, servant, wages, farm, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Moser把一年的工钱说定，管吃住，冬天加一双鞋。Hedwig把铺盖卷搬进长工屋，草垫上还留着上一个人的汗味。Josef Bauer端来一碗热汤，说夜里冷就多盖一层麻袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (长工屋里的草垫); food; flag +hired_as_farm_servant; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation friend with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Hedwig傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Franz Lechner在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Franz Lechner敲开Hedwig的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-franz-lechner; mood 0.04999999999999999_

  “十克朗押金，先交后干。”Friedrich Winter 2说得很平。Hedwig把两只口袋翻给人家看，柜台上只落下一张皱车票。工头收回话头，他也不再问，提着帽子走出栅栏。

- **1936.03** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Hedwig把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Maria Berg站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Hedwig手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  Hedwig在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Hedwig拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Anton找到Hedwig时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1937.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Ferdinand Huber 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1939.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.qualification-exam/p10-edu-work-tasks.qualification-exam.partial` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, exam
  簿记和算术过了，速记差两分。Elisabeth Berg在成绩单上分栏注明，只发单科证明，明年可以补考速记。他把单科证明折好，知道商号要的是整张。回厂时Josef Moser问他考得怎样，他说差一点。
  _effects: cash -5 (报名费不退); skill; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +shorthand_retake; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1939.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Josef Stein 2把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Hedwig打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1939.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Anna Moser 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1939.11** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Hedwig看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000257:child; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Hedwig最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Hedwig把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Hedwig说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 34 -> 40; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 40 -> 37; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.cheap-picture` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Hedwig和Friedrich Kral买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Hedwig把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1940.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铜板一枚枚落在桌面上，比说好的少了十五赫勒。Hedwig没去捡，先问Mitzi Stein下捆还送不送。Mitzi Stein含糊应了一声，拎起布包就走。Gustav Hofer这才进来，说下回得先讲好价。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1940.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-pot-empty` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  锅底刮出焦味，Elisabeth Berg 3说今天就这些了，明天赶早。Hedwig把空罐子夹在腋下往回走，石板路上结了薄冰，Otto Berg 2在拐角等着，二话没说把罐子递过来。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1940.12** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Hedwig和Rosa Bauer合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1941.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Katharina Stein的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Stein今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 29 -> 37; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 64 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.hired` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Hedwig在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Franz Lechner把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。
  _effects: cash 55 (首月工钱扣除保证金后实发); skill; employment 保险写字间试用办事员 @ org-wien-magistrat; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner_

- **1941.02** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/the-comb-snaps` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, vienna
  梳子断了一根齿，Elisabeth Bauer说早就该换；Hedwig说还能用。两个人为了谁忘了买煤争了几句，最后Elisabeth Bauer把湿头发扎起来，坐在窗边不说话。Hedwig把断梳子放进抽屉，晚上又拿出来，试着梳自己的头发。
  _effects: relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 74 -> 71; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +broken_comb_quarrel; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1941.06** `r3-war.occupied-bells::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/surrendered-copper` tags: war, occupation, requisition, bells, resistance, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig带人把铜锅、门把手和教堂的烛台堆在广场上，Elisabeth Berg 3逐个过秤。Berta Weiss 2想把曾祖父的怀表留下，被Hedwig按住手；Anna Bauer把自己的铜十字架也放进堆里，说“称这个的时候轻一点”。
  _effects: cash -4 (钟楼上的征用令); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +surrendered_copper_to_occupier; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation friend with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  Elisabeth Hofer 2在葬礼上偷拍了一张Hedwig靠着棺材睡着的照片，说只要不给四克朗，就把照片寄给Hedwig的老板。Hedwig还没有凑钱，照片先被家里人选去做了悼念卡；亲戚们说，只有真正伤心的人才会累成这样。Elisabeth Hofer 2拿着底片站在门口，第一次不知道该威胁谁。
  _effects: cash 3 (相馆支付的悼念卡照片使用费); reputation 0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; condition; flag +blackmail_photo_became_mourning_card; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

  警察在午夜后闯进地下舞厅，把灯全部打开。Elisabeth Berg 3让所有人靠墙站好，逐个记下名字和住址；Hedwig看见Franz Stein的鞋跟断了一只。第二天，名单上的一些名字出现在报纸的“风化事件”栏里。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:646; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 46 -> 66; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 34 -> 19; reputation -0.1; cash -2 (a fine and a broken shoe); flag +name_in_police_notebook; flag +public_desire_active_

  办公室有人问Hedwig，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Hedwig说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Hedwig把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 66 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 19 -> 15; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  Hedwig和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Hedwig在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 71 -> 75; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 74 -> 71; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig在三月最后一个星期一清出那张空工作台，用铅笔在接缝处划了三条线，一块榆木抽屉板刨到发亮。Anna Bauer用拇指沿燕尾榫摸过去，没说话，只把评语写在纸上：榫严，面平，可出师。Hedwig向柜上付了两克朗考试费，把证明折好放进内袋，下周起工钱按伙计算。
  _effects: cash -2 (出师考试的考试费与材料费); skill; reputation 0.05; relation employer with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Stein这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 37 -> 40; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 40 -> 35; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 61 -> 66; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +directed_refugee_to_relief; knowledge “the relief office opens at seven and does not ask for a resi”_

  Hedwig发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Anna Moser 2问是谁动的。Anna Moser 2说是Franz Lechner嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1913.01** `vienna-1912-orphan-guardianship/guardian-confirmed` tags: childhood, bereavement, guardianship, orphan, poor_relief
  Friedrich Hofer 在市政办事处核对 Anton 和 Klara 的死亡登记，确认 Theresia 自1909年起实际抚养 Marie。他签发监护确认书，并批给每月六克朗贫民补助。Marie 继续住在同一张床上，学籍保留。
  _effects: cash 6 (monthly poor relief for the guardian); food; relation guardian with person-marie-huber; role_

- **1913.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Grete Stein从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Marie Huber答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Marie Huber回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Grete Stein看。

  _effects: skill; mood -0.03_

  _effects: reputation 0.02; mood 0.05; flag +rang_the_school_bell_

  _effects: cash -3 (琴房里漏风的窗); role; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein_

- **1915.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning

- **1921.12** `recipe-education.scholarship-exam::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/cheated` tags: education, exam, class, poverty, ambition, university, culture, schooling

  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +cheated_scholarship_exam; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1922.01** `r13-edu.library-study::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/copies-whole-chapter` tags: education, library, study, reading, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Marie Huber把借书证压在阅览室的台灯下，一页页抄书。Mitzi Stein过来提醒闭馆，Marie Huber求了十分钟，把最后一章抄完。回家路上，Marie Huber把纸条贴在胸口，怕雪打湿，进门先烤干手才敢展开。第二天Marie Huber把抄本交给老师，老师翻了两页，说字比课文好看。
  _effects: flag +library_card; skill; knowledge “the library holds an 1890s survey of housing conditions”_

- **1922.04** `recipe-education.scholarship-exam::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/passed` tags: education, exam, class, poverty, ambition, university, culture, schooling
  Marie Huber用Elisabeth Bauer 2借的钢笔写完最后一题，笔尖在纸上划出一道细缝。放榜那天，Franz Lechner 2把Marie Huber的名字念了两遍，Elisabeth Bauer 2站在人群后面，先鼓掌，再低头看自己的鞋。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; education secondary_

- **1922.06** `r14-edu.matriculation::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/deferred-and-clerked` tags: education, university_admission, class_shame, university, culture, schooling, student, research
  Marie Huber的分数够了，家里却拿不出注册费。Mitzi Stein说可以保留学籍一年，Marie Huber没有再来。秋天，Marie Huber在市政厅找到一份抄写员的活，桌子靠窗，能看见大学钟楼的尖顶。午休时，Marie Huber把带来的旧课本翻开，又合上。抄写员月薪四克朗。
  _effects: cash 4 (注册处窗口的旧外套); flag +university_deferred; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1923.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Marie Huber上一个东家是谁。Marie Huber说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Marie Huber被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Marie Huber站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1923.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Franz Lechner的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Franz Lechner算了一夜，第二天把Marie Huber送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery)_

- **1923.01** `r13-edu.teacher-training::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university::reform-pedagogy/caterpillar-lesson` tags: education, teacher_training, pedagogy, work, certification, university, culture, schooling
  Marie Huber在改革后的师范学校学新教学法，Anton Kral让Marie Huber带学生去公园上自然课。Marie Huber蹲在草地上教孩子认叶脉，一个男孩把毛毛虫放在Marie Huber手背上。Marie Huber没有甩开，就着那条虫子讲完了整节课，下课才发现自己跪得膝盖发麻。裤脚全是草屑。
  _effects: flag +reform_teacher_training; role; education higher; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1923.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Anna Huber先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Marie Huber点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Marie Huber把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  Marie Huber考得不错，Mitzi Stein把分数记在名册上，说位置可以给他，但保证金十五克朗要先交，还得有铺保。他手头只有十克朗出头，保人一时也找不到。椅子给了下一位，那人进门时袖口已经熨得笔直。Marie Huber把答卷折好带走，纸边被汗浸软了。
  _effects: cash -1 (往返内城的车钱与考试杂费); mood -0.04999999999999999; reputation -0.009999999999999995_

  Marie Huber把通知念给Theresia Huber听，Theresia Huber摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Marie Huber去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-anna-moser_

  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.03999999999999998; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; skill_

  _effects: cash -10 (新城区门牌下的招租纸); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +lodging_key_money_paid; knowledge “the previous lodger’s furniture was held for key money”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Marie Huber的试讲被督学打断，督学说新方法太散，还是背书写字牢靠。Marie Huber没有争辩，在课后把两种教案都写了一遍，附上学生的作业对比。督学看完，没有表态，只把材料带走了，Marie Huber在门口站到天黑。教案被留在督学的公文包里。

  Marie Huber把“蓝多瑙”看成了“蓝星期一”，在柜台前把五克朗押在一匹没人看好的马上。最后一道弯，那匹马从外道切进来，Karl Kraus手里的铅笔停在票面上。赔率是一赔十二；Anna Huber把赢来的钱数了两遍，Marie Huber还在看自己的票，像不相信上面的名字。
  _effects: cash 60 (winnings from the wrong horse at long odds); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:272; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; reputation 0.03; flag +wrong_horse_won; mood 0.08999999999999997; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Marie Huber把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Marie Huber就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Marie Huber的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Marie Huber家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Berta Hofer进门后没有先翻账本，而是问Marie Huber是不是在战前服过役。两个人对上了部队编号，Berta Hofer把帽子放在桌上，说利息可以按小额储蓄申报，罚款就免了。Luise Bauer松了一口气，手里的计算尺却还停在半空，像是不知道该不该落下去。
  _effects: cash -2 (the reduced tax assessment); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 50 -> 48; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82; flag +tax_audit_friendly; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Marie Huber把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Marie Huber来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  半夜，Emil Weiss敲开楼门，说有人看见生人进了这栋楼。Marie Huber说阁楼堆着煤，没人上去。Emil Weiss还是上去看了一圈，靴子踩得楼板吱响。人走后，Marie Huber发现Franz Huber把煤箱盖从里面顶住了。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (阁楼上的脚步声); reputation -0.05000000000000002; condition; flag +police_searched_attic; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; knowledge “the police searched the attic but did not move the coal box”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1934.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Marie Huber记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Luise Weiss说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Luise Weiss陪着Marie Huber去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Marie Huber把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 48 -> 43; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Luise Bauer 2在门房里听完，从抽屉里摸出一张印着厂名的信纸，写Marie Huber“手脚干净，肯学机器”，又签了名。墨水没干，他用吸墨纸按了按，说别拿去给太大的厂，小厂才看这个。Marie Huber把信折成四折，放进内袋最里面。
  _effects: cash 1 (旧工头的一封荐书); flag +work13_obtained_reference_letter; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; knowledge “the old foreman still has letterhead from the factory”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  Marie Huber没有立刻答话，先去敲Anna Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anna Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Marie Huber把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1934.05** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Marie Huber把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Franz Lechner递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (屋里整天没生旺火，受冻); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner_

  _effects: cash -2 (登记簿上的注销线); condition; flag +divorce_refused_for_lack_of_evidence; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; knowledge “divorce requires proof of adultery or desertion”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

  师傅Anton Weiss 2把一块冷毛巾塞进Marie Huber手里，让Marie Huber先擦死者的脸，再擦自己的。殡葬合作社的规矩写在门背后：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块写着日期的木牌。Marie Huber做完第一具，手抖得点不着烟。

  Marie Huber让Mitzi Lechner睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Marie Huber把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); food; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +kin_staying_over_

  Marie Huber陪Hermann Weiss去市政办事处，要求重新验尸，并写下药粉的来路。办事员翻着本子，说死亡证明写的是心力衰竭，可死者前一天拿过一包白色药粉；说会转给卫生局，但得等。Marie Huber把药包的事又说了一遍，出门时天已经黑了，回信还没有日子。

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Marie Huber的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Marie Huber想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  Anna Bauer退休前把一箱琴谱交给Marie Huber，说里面有些是自己抄的，错音不少，别照着弹。Marie Huber翻到一本旧练习曲，页边有铅笔写的指法，还有几处被橡皮擦破的洞。Anna Bauer说这些谱子跟了自己四十年，现在该换个地方落灰了。箱底还有三克朗纸币。

- **1945.07** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Mitzi Berg 2用膝盖顶住，Marie Huber把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1945.08** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/wait-with-wet-cloth` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Marie Huber用湿布和半瓶退烧药熬到天亮，炉子多烧了一筐煤，锅里的粥也多添了两把面，口粮下去一截。Rosa Stein 3来时只在门口站了站，留一张饮食单，说继续观察，出诊费免了。Marie Huber整夜没合眼，Berta 2的烧慢慢退下去；桌上多了一张纸，炉边少了一筐煤。

  Marie Huber的书印了三年，封面是灰色的，印数八百册。Emil Weiss在书店橱窗里看见，进去买了一本，让Marie Huber在扉页上签名。Marie Huber写到一半停下，问Emil Weiss还记不记得地下室那只樟木箱。Emil Weiss说记得，箱底还有半包樟脑。版税六克朗，Marie Huber请Emil Weiss吃了顿饭。
  _effects: cash 6 (注脚里的小城); reputation 0.12; flag +archive_book_published; role; relation colleague with person-emil-weiss_

  Marie Huber跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Marie Huber的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。

  _effects: organization_membership; role_

  Franz Lechner没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Franz Lechner把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Franz Lechner说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。

  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); mood -0.039999999999999994_

  Franz Lechner把日记本摊在Alois Lechner面前，小铜锁还挂在搭扣上，锁鼻却被撬歪了。Franz Lechner翻到多出来的那一行字：今天什么都没发生。Alois Lechner说只是担心，Franz Lechner说担心不是理由。Mitzi Lechner在门口说不是自己写的，Alois Lechner让他闭嘴。那天晚饭，桌上只有刀叉的声音，Franz Lechner把撬坏的锁放在桌角，谁也没去碰。

  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Franz Lechner用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Karl Kraus站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Franz Lechner看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); residence_

- **1918.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Emil Weiss在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Franz Lechner把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1918.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Anna Moser凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Franz Lechner把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family

  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_

  _effects: cash -14 (退票手续费、去汉堡的火车票与两天排队住店); mood -0.08; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  邻居过来看了一眼，教Franz Lechner用湿布敷额。Mitzi天亮时安静睡了，Franz Lechner却耽误半日活计；一克朗五十赫勒的工钱没了，床边的醋味却留了一整天。
  _effects: health 0.05 (家庭护理后烧略退); cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱); health -0.03 (整夜未眠的疲惫); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1919.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Franz Lechner说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Anna Moser没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Franz Lechner听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; flag +funeral_society_left_

  走到半山就落雨，三人挤进酒农的棚子，把篮子里的面包和盐分完，吃食折合一克朗。Josef Bauer把外套顶在头上先跑，Helene Weiss与Franz Lechner留在最后收篮子；雨点打在葡萄叶上，Franz Lechner的鞋袜湿到傍晚，脚趾发麻，谁也没急着走。
  _effects: food; health -0.01999999999999999 (淋了雨，鞋袜湿到傍晚); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  Franz Lechner在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anna Bauer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +manuscript_lost_in_mail; knowledge “lost manuscripts end up in the basement sorting room”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  车夫把价钱咬得很死，Franz Lechner还到五克朗又添了二十赫勒，才说定跑一趟。Emil Weiss扶着床架，车过路口时煤箱歪了一下，细灰撒在路面上。到了地方，东西卸在门厅，天已经大亮。
  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱); residence; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000001; flag +public_health_survey; knowledge “seven households shared one water tap in the lane”_

  _effects: cash -1 (预科班的最后一个名额); flag +admitted_with_certificate_copy; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; knowledge “the original school records were damaged by water”; education secondary_

  Franz Lechner坐在阶梯教室最后一排，把Anton Kral说的每个书名都记下来。讲到一半，Anton Kral忽然问谁能复述刚才的论证，教室里没人出声。Franz Lechner小声答了一句，Anton Kral让Franz Lechner大声些，然后接着讲，粉笔在黑板上断成两截。下课后Franz Lechner把断掉的粉笔头捡起来，夹进笔记本，当作书签。

  _effects: cash -2 (学位袍下的补丁); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +graduated_without_post; role; relation rival with person-luise-kral; education higher_

  Maria Berg连着三个星期日没有回来。Franz Lechner去问工头，工头说Maria Berg跟着一批季节工去了下奥地利，铺盖已经托人搬进合租房。Franz Lechner把Maria Berg的靴子卖了八克朗，用这笔钱付了房租，又把Maria Berg的工票收进抽屉。门房问要不要留话，Franz Lechner说不用，顺手把门牌上的灰擦了。
  _effects: household; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; role; flag +deserted_by_partner; cash 8 (sold the partner's boots to pay the rent); mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1924.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Franz Lechner听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Hermann Kral扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.06000000000000005 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1924.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Franz Lechner熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1925.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Franz Lechner在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Franz Lechner把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001820_

- **1925.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/house-call-doctor-arrives` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, rural, agriculture, small_town
  医生在午后进门，先看了看炉子，再听胸口。他说是肺炎，留下三包药粉和一张账单；Hedwig Stein替Franz Lechner把医生送到门口，回来时把药按顿分好。邻居把账单压在钟底下，说这钱先别急着还，药要按时吃。
  _effects: cash -12 (house call, medicine powder and a certificate); health 0.040000000000000036 (pneumonia treated at home by a doctor); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-hedwig-stein; knowledge “医生出诊先看炉子再听胸口，三包药粉和一张账单就是全部治疗。”_

- **1926.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Franz Lechner在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Luise Bauer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1926.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/half-scholarship` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, rural, agriculture, small_town
  校长看了Maria Lechner的算术本，说可以免掉一半学费。Franz Lechner在办公室里坐了很久，反复确认“不是借的”；出来时，Maria Lechner正在操场上追一只皮球，鞋底已经磨穿了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 55 -> 38; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 32; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 65; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 2 (the first instalment of the school grant); reputation 0.03_

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.recommendation::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/recommendation-secured` tags: education, recommendation, teacher, application, favor, university, culture, schooling
  Anton Kral把信纸铺开，问Franz Lechner想申请哪里。Franz Lechner说完，Anton Kral写了三页，最后一句是“此人有独立判断”。Franz Lechner站在旁边，看见Anton Kral把笔尖蘸了两次墨水，心里比考试还紧张，出门才发现自己一直屏着气。那封信Franz Lechner抄了一份留着，原件寄出前看了三遍。
  _effects: flag +recommendation_secured; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1926.12** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family, injury
  四克朗放在桌上，Franz Lechner数了一遍，又添进房租的信封。孩子半夜翻来覆去，学徒在厨房里咳嗽；Franz Lechner睁着眼到天亮，眼下青了一圈。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); health -0.040000000000000036 (overcrowded housing); condition_

  Franz Lechner蹲在门口把Maria Lechner的书包带系紧，又把一块面包塞进侧袋。学校里挤了七十多个孩子，Franz Stein按名字点名，点到Maria Lechner时抬头看了一眼。Franz Lechner在走廊外等到铃响，听见里面开始念字母，才把攥皱的报名纸收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包带系紧一点); flag +child_enrolled; role; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1927.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Franz Lechner傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Berta Kral在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Berta Kral敲开Franz Lechner的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  鱼头用报纸包着，报纸上还印着昨天的广告。Emil Weiss一边刮冰一边说，回去多搁姜，别舍不得醋。Franz Lechner把纸包拎在手里，能感觉到凉气从指缝里钻出来，走到家时报纸已经洇湿了一层。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Rosa Bauer在几天后找到Franz Lechner，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。

  Rosa Bauer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Grete Lechner。Franz Lechner在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Franz Lechner从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:136_

  Franz Lechner在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Katharina Weiss放学后跟着Franz Lechner去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:137; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000414:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +advertised_for_the_childs_medal; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; flag +something_is_missing_

  Luise Kral在阁楼的煤箱后面躲了三天，Franz Lechner每天夜里送一次面包和水。第三天，楼下有人问起生人味，Franz Lechner说那是煤烟。Luise Kral走时，把一枚磨亮的纽扣留在窗台上。
  _effects: cash -1 (阁楼上的脚步声); food; flag +hid_fugitive_three_nights; knowledge “the fugitive crossed the yard before the morning roll call”_

- **1928.10** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/correct-it-twice` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, reform
  Franz Lechner在窗口要求更正，Gustav Berg把表格翻了三遍，最后开出一张新的出生证明，名字却少了一个字母。于是城里同时住着一个死人、一个活人，和一个只在档案里出生的陌生人。Franz Lechner拿着两张纸站在区公所门口，忽然不知道该用哪一张去领下个月的煤票。
  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:138; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Franz Lechner手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

  一个税务员把Franz Lechner的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Franz Lechner收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Franz Lechner先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 40 -> 47; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 25; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `bc18-ghost.own-funeral::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government/lets-it-run` tags: bureaucracy, funeral, mistaken_identity, public, absurd, death, humiliation, reform
  Franz Lechner没有进门，只站在酒馆对面的雨棚下。Hedwig Stein在里面念悼词，说死者一生慷慨、爱吃甜、从不欠账；Franz Lechner听见这些不属于自己的好话，忽然哭了起来。捐款盘里收了六克朗，Hedwig Stein把它们包进手帕。Franz Lechner等到灯灭才走，雨已经停了。
  _effects: cash -1 (在雨棚下买的热酒); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 25 -> 30; relation kin with person-hedwig-stein; knowledge “远亲记得的并不是我欠过的账，而是我爱吃甜。”; condition; flag +watched_own_wake; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 30 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 47 -> 54; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 42 -> 52; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: mood 0.05999999999999994; flag +foreign_language_certificate; skill_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Franz Lechner把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Elisabeth Winter没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Franz Lechner回头看了一眼，Elisabeth Winter正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1931.01** `bc18-ghost.own-funeral::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lets-it-run` tags: bureaucracy, funeral, mistaken_identity, public, absurd, death, humiliation, reform
  Franz Lechner没有进门，只站在酒馆对面的雨棚下。Anton Huber 2在里面念悼词，说死者一生慷慨、爱吃甜、从不欠账；Franz Lechner听见这些不属于自己的好话，忽然哭了起来。捐款盘里收了六克朗，Anton Huber 2把它们包进手帕。Franz Lechner等到灯灭才走，雨已经停了。
  _effects: cash -1 (在雨棚下买的热酒); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 57; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; knowledge “远亲记得的并不是我欠过的账，而是我爱吃甜。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Franz Lechner的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Franz Lechner收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Franz Lechner先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 54 -> 61; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 57 -> 67; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Franz Lechner把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Franz Lechner忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 8 -> 16; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 8 -> 6; mood -0.009999999999999787_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.client-list/p9-work-career.client-list.referral` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, clients, reputation
  咖啡馆后厨的蒸汽把窗玻璃熏白了一层，Franz Lechner在蒸汽里上蜡，袖口沾了蜡痕。临走时老板在账单背面写了个地址，说杂货铺老板姓什么他一时想不起。
  _effects: cash 4 (修咖啡馆八把椅子的工钱); reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; skill_

- **1931.03** `p9-work-transport.strike-rumor/strike-rumor-keep-quiet` tags: work, factory, railway, transport, shift, labour, strike, rumor
  Franz Lechner什么也没说，照常上工。罢工那天厂门口只来了一半人，汽笛拉过两遍，车间里的皮带挂着没动。Elisabeth Berg在门房里点名，把到的人记下，把没到的人也记下。Franz Lechner站在车床前，听见外面远处的喊声，手里的活没停。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1931.10** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Marie Huber排在前面，回头替Franz Lechner看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Franz Lechner用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government/half-scholarship` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, reform, medical, schooling
  校长看了Anton Lechner的算术本，说可以免掉一半学费。Franz Lechner在办公室里坐了很久，反复确认“不是借的”；出来时，Anton Lechner正在操场上追一只皮球，鞋底已经磨穿了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 40 -> 38; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 53 -> 32; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 87 -> 65; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 5 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 2 (the first instalment of the school grant); reputation 0.02999999999999997_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  汤勺在锅里搅了两圈，Franz Lechner把熏肉块拨到Mitzi Lechner碗里。热气模糊了窗玻璃，街上的圣诞摊子传来铃铛声；Mitzi Lechner吃得很慢，碗底最后一点汤也用面包擦干净。
  _effects: food; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Franz Stein翻到报名纸背面，说还缺一张出生证明和一双完整的鞋。Franz Lechner低头看Maria Lechner脚上那双露脚趾的鞋，说下周补上；Franz Stein把纸推回来，说名额只留到星期五。回家的路上，Maria Lechner一直问学校是什么样子。
  _effects: cash -1 (书包带系紧一点); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +school_enrolment_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; condition_

- **1932.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Franz Lechner刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Mitzi Weiss 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Mitzi Weiss 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1933.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Weiss 2嫌Franz Lechner的床单滴水滴到Mitzi Weiss 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Franz Lechner下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Franz Lechner把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi Weiss 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004755_

  Franz Lechner没有给Maria Lechner报名。家里需要一双能看摊的手，学校也还欠着上一学期的纸笔钱；晚上Maria Lechner把别人的课本借来看，Franz Lechner假装没有看见。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 8; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 6; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> false; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 1 (the child's small earnings at the stall); flag +child_kept_from_school_

- **1933.02** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Franz Lechner和Anton Hofer谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Franz Lechner家，钥匙只在白天给。Anton Hofer说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Mitzi Stein 2听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Franz Lechner把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.04** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Franz Lechner挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Marie Huber敲门时，Franz Lechner从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996_

  Anna Moser 2在墙外念了一段经，没有穿祭披。Franz Lechner把木匣放进土里，看见旁边还有三个没有名字的小坑。Josef Stein 2递来一把野花，说这墙根冬天也能晒到太阳。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; condition; flag +buried_infant_outside_church_wall; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “three other unnamed infants lie along the same wall”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Franz Lechner被叫到学校，老师说Anton Lechner这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Franz Lechner没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 59 -> 64; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1935.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/care-sick-parent.hospital` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, rural
  Franz Lechner把Alois Lechner送进市立医院，Alois Lechner不肯，抓住床栏说死也要死在家里。Franz Lechner还是签了字；病房里六张床，Alois Lechner被安排在靠门的一张，探视时间一到，护士就把家属往外赶。签字笔是借来的，笔尖分叉，名字写得比平时大。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; health 0.04 (hospital treatment and supervision); cash -14 (hospital deposit and ward fees); role; flag +parent_in_hospital; knowledge “市立医院每天只许探视一小时。”_

- **1935.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa 2这周主动擦了黑板。Franz Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006424_

- **1935.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Franz Lechner在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Luise Kral从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤)_

- **1935.10** `r1-civ.meeting-ban-order::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ban-pasted-on-door` tags: politics, police, repression, meeting, organization, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Stein比开会的人早到半小时，把一张禁止集会的告示贴在门板上，浆糊顺着木纹往下淌。Gustav Berg 2说屋子已经租到月底，钥匙却已经被收走。Alois Lechner让众人散到街对面，Franz Lechner站在告示前，把上面的法令编号抄了下来。
  _effects: flag +meeting_banned_before_opening; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the ban cites a specific emergency decree number”_

- **1935.11** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Franz Lechner没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 7 -> 8; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 64 -> 62; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006428_

- **1935.12** `r13-rel.proposal-accepted::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university::proposal-accepted-war/sewed-the-ring-into-the-lining` tags: relationship, courtship, engagement, family, marriage, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Weiss 3的征召令在订婚宴后第三天送到。两家人把红纸收起来，Franz Lechner连夜替Mitzi Weiss 3缝了一件衬里，把订婚戒指缝在左胸口袋。Mitzi Weiss 3上车时没有回头，Franz Lechner一直站到月台空了。
  _effects: condition; role; knowledge “the betrothal ring is sewn into the left breast pocket”; move place-wien-printshop-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1935.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Franz Lechner还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 5 -> 7; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006194_

- **1936.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Franz Lechner把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Luise Kral抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Franz Lechner把床单对折，腾出一半，Luise Kral用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006197_

- **1936.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Franz Lechner没有立刻答话，先去敲Gustav Hofer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Gustav Hofer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Franz Lechner把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anton Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Franz Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Franz Lechner把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。

  半年过去，Franz Lechner又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 5 -> 11; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 45 -> 43; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/name-on-list` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner替Mitzi Stein把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Berta Hofer翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Mitzi Stein听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Franz Lechner替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

  Franz Lechner说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Maria Berg没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Franz Lechner听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1938.01** `p9-work-career.reference/p9-work-career.reference.bare` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, reference, certificate
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien照着账本核了一遍，提笔只写“已离职，结清工钱，再无欠款”，落款盖了圆章，纸上大半是空白。Franz Lechner拿着这张纸走了两条街，在环城路的柱子旁读了两遍。新东家那边人事科的窗口收下它，没有多问，也没有叫他进去谈。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.07** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Franz Lechner在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Franz Lechner时停住。晚上，Franz Lechner把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 45 -> 55; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007865_

- **1938.09** `p9-history-medical-daily.house-call/wait-with-wet-cloth` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily, injury
  Franz Lechner用湿布和半瓶退烧药熬到天亮，炉子多烧了一筐煤，锅里的粥也多添了两把面，口粮下去一截。Elisabeth Berg 3来时只在门口站了站，留一张饮食单，说继续观察，出诊费免了。Franz Lechner整夜没合眼，Otto Berg 2的烧慢慢退下去；桌上多了一张纸，炉边少了一筐煤。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (整夜守候，睡眠不足); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1938.12** `r3-mig.colonial-return::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/trunk-and-quinine` tags: migration, colonial, return, land, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Weiss从殖民地回来，皮箱上贴着轮船公司的标签，里面是发霉的衬衫、一包奎宁和几张没有盖章的地契。Franz Lechner把床让出来，Otto Weiss说那边热得连影子都短。夜里他咳嗽，Franz Lechner起来给他倒水。
  _effects: cash -3 (从殖民地回来的皮箱); food; condition; flag +took_in_returning_colonist; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “the returnee has land deeds with no stamps”_

- **1938.12** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Franz Lechner还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007823_

- **1938.12** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa 2这周主动擦了黑板。Franz Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 42 -> 45; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008060_

- **1939.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/wrong-train` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Lechner上错了火车，半夜又提着别人的箱子回来。箱子里是一套戏服和一顶礼帽；Franz Lechner没有骂，只把箱子送到车站，回来时Maria Lechner已经趴在桌上睡着了。
  _effects: flag +wrong_train_suitcase; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 32 -> 35; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Franz Lechner没有立刻答话，先去敲Gustav Berg 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Gustav Berg 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Franz Lechner把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Franz Lechner当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 70 -> 80; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008435_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Grete Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Franz Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 26; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008661_

  Franz Lechner把Grete Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Franz Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 26 -> 34; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 62 -> 59; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Franz Lechner把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Franz Lechner忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 63 -> 71; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 3 -> 1; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `task-vienna-medical-work.poor-patient/poor-patient-parish` tags: health, medicine, hospital, care, knowledge, profession, task, task:health
  Franz Lechner陪着Gustav Berg 2从互助会出来，药瓶已经装在纸袋里。拇指上的红印还没擦干净，路上有熟人问起，Gustav Berg 2把手往袖子里缩了缩。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +parish_aid_arranged_

- **1939.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Mitzi Weiss的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Mitzi Weiss在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Franz Lechner下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Mitzi Weiss端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000540:child_

- **1939.07** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/license-it-and-keep-the-patent` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner没有卖断，而是和Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien签了按瓶抽成的许可：每卖出一千瓶，付两克朗。第一年只卖了四千瓶，Franz Lechner拿到八克朗，却保住了专利。Berta Weiss 2在许可书上盖了一个小章，说这种条款以后会值钱；Franz Lechner把章看了一会儿，没敢笑。
  _effects: cash 8 (first royalty payment on the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:514; skill; flag +licensed_patent; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Katharina Kral在午休时把本子摊在Franz Lechner的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Franz Lechner从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Katharina Kral当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1940.01** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Gustav Berg 2用膝盖顶住，Franz Lechner把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.050000000000000044 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛)_

- **1940.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  二楼的老木匠指着裂开的踏板骂起来。Franz Lechner手里还捏着念珠，Gustav Hofer 2挡在中间说木板本来就朽了。房东太太在门后听着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1940.01** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Otto Berg 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Franz Lechner把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Otto Berg 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/letter-returned` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  汇款单被退了回来，理由是收件地址“查无此人”。Franz Lechner拿着那张盖了退件章的纸去问邮局，又去问同乡会；没有人知道那家人搬去了哪里，只有邮局墙上的钟还在走，秒针每一下都像在提醒钱没有到。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 80 -> 86; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +mb17_remittance_letter_returned; knowledge “退件章意味着钱没有到，也意味着那个地址已经失效。”_

  Franz Lechner把Maria Lechner的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria Lechner今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Franz Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

  半年过去，Franz Lechner又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 1 -> 7; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 71 -> 69; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Franz Lechner把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 20 -> 15; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Elisabeth Berg把两人的名字念了一遍，问还有没有挽回的余地。Franz Lechner说没有，Franz Huber 2也没有出声。Elisabeth Berg在登记簿上画了一道注销线，收走八克朗的手续费；走出办事处时，Franz Huber 2往左，Franz Lechner往右，谁也没有回头。
  _effects: cash -8 (登记簿上的注销线); condition; flag +divorce_granted; role; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; knowledge “the marriage was struck from the register”_

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Franz Lechner去参加了家长会，把中奖的票据、邮局收据和那张登了地址的报纸一起放在桌上，说钱是运气，不是偷的。没有人鼓掌，但也没有人再提转学。Grete Lechner在门外等，书包带子绕在手指上；回家路上，Franz Lechner第一次听见孩子主动说起学校的事。
  _effects: cash -5 (the parents' meeting and a small donation to the school); state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 69 -> 77; reputation -0.010000000000000009; flag +child_stayed_in_school; mood 0.030000000000000027_
