  Franz Lechner被叫到学校，老师说Anton Lechner这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Franz Lechner没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 51 -> 46; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 58 -> 63; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Franz Lechner的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:444; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Franz Lechner把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 15 -> 10; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 5 -> 10; cash -1 (the account does not balance)_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.exhibit-painting::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/painting-ignored` tags: culture, painting, exhibition, art, critic, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Lechner把画挂在画廊最里面的墙角，标签上只写了“无题”。三天里，来看的人从画前走过去，停在旁边的风景画前。闭展时，Franz Lechner把画取下来，画框背面落了一层灰。
  _effects: flag +painting_ignored_at_exhibition_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Grete Lechner这周主动擦了黑板。Franz Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 29 -> 32; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010094_

- **1941.02** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Franz Lechner没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 8 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 86 -> 84; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009868_

- **1941.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Franz Lechner把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Franz Lechner拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1941.04** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Franz Lechner在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Katharina Weiss 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Franz Lechner把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1941.04** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Franz Lechner还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009783_

- **1941.04** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Franz Lechner家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 3; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009783_

- **1941.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Franz Lechner把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Elisabeth Hofer 2提着空碗上来时，只看见Franz Lechner把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1942.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/takes-the-train` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Lechner把行李捆在自行车后座上，说要去城里找活。Franz Lechner往包里塞了两个面包和一双旧袜子，没有送到车站；火车开走以后，Franz Lechner才发现自己一直站在原地数车厢。
  _effects: move place-wien; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome true -> true; cash 1 (one fewer mouth at the table); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/foster-refused` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner看了看自家两张床和已经见底的粮袋，说实在添不了一口人。Otto Weiss没有劝，只把Elisabeth Huber的手拉紧，转身下楼。Franz Lechner站在门口，听见那双小鞋在楼梯上一下一下地响，响到二楼才停。
  _effects: flag +refused_to_foster; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1942.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Franz Lechner发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Friedrich Stein问是谁动的。Friedrich Stein说是Grete Huber 2嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1942.04** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/hand-over-the-grain` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Alois Stein把征用单按在柜台上，Franz Lechner家里的两袋面粉被划走一袋，换回一张盖蓝章的收据。Alois Stein说下周还会来，如果地窖里再翻出粮食，收据就不作数了。
  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价); food; flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”_

- **1942.12** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Friedrich Winter从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Franz Lechner买双能进厂的结实靴子。Franz Lechner数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴)_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Franz Lechner的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Franz Lechner看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007825_

- **1943.01** `hs18-blackout.cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hide-the-coal` tags: blackout, cellar, coal, neighbor, denunciation, history, vienna, urban
  Franz Lechner在地窖最里面看见两桶煤，桶盖上压着Helene Winter 2家的旧毯子。Helene Winter 2说这是给病人留的；楼上的巡逻队正在敲第一扇门。Franz Lechner把毯子重新铺平，又把自家的空桶挪到前面。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:663; flag +hid_neighbor_coal; health -0.010000000000000009 (coal dust in the cellar)_

- **1943.01** `r13-fam.join-club::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::club-crisis/joined-under-watch` tags: task, social, club, choir, organization, belonging, vienna, urban
  排练到一半，两个便衣推门进来，站在最后一排听。Hermann Weiss没有停，只把一首哀歌换成进行曲。散场后，Hermann Weiss让Franz Lechner把点名册带回家藏一夜，说Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens里最近多了几个不唱歌只记名字的人。
  _effects: flag +carried_choir_roll_home; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; organization_membership; knowledge “plainclothes men attend the choir rehearsals”_

- **1943.02** `statistical_infection`
  Franz Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1943.05** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-hand-to-union` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Franz Lechner把请愿书递给工会代表，自己退到后排靠柱子的位置。Marie Huber从旁边递来一支短铅笔，让Franz Lechner把台上说的数目记在请柬背面，回家再誊清楚。
  _effects: skill_

- **1943.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Otto Weiss点了牛奶咖啡，Franz Lechner要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.09** `state_reaction:hs18-street-witness/interrogation-pressure` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  又有人来问那天的事。Franz Lechner把同一段话重说了一遍，对方却只记住其中两个名字；走的时候，门没有关严，楼道里的风把纸吹得直响。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 40 -> 50_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010858_

- **1943.11** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Berg 3把黑皮本翻到Franz Lechner那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Hedwig在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Franz Lechner当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 30 -> 22; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; flag +taxes_paid; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.11** `statistical_death/tuberculosis` tags: death
  Franz Lechner咳了几个月，手帕上开始带血。1943年冬天，Franz Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的床上消瘦下去，再没有力气去上工。Elisabeth Bauer在场。
  _effects: death (肺结核)_

- **1913.01** `kernel-migrate.pension-appeal::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/civic-pension-granted` tags: elderhood, pension, bureaucracy, family, rural, vienna, urban, peace
  她带来一叠发黄的工资单，用线缝在一起。Friedrich Hofer数到第三张时抬起头，说“这些可以算”；一个月后，第一笔养老金汇到，她把钱分成房租、药费和给孙子的糖。她把汇款单压在桌玻璃下，旁边是丈夫的旧照片。

- **1919.10** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.pension-window/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.pension-window.missing-page` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, bureaucracy, cash
  Gustav Berg在窗口里翻到第三遍，说Theresia Huber的工资册缺了一页，缺的那年工龄对不上，这个月只能先按低档发。窗口递出来一张纸条，要她下周带着原雇主或见证人的证明再来；Anna Moser凑近看了一眼，说自己在同一家厂干过，可以替她说话。
  _effects: cash 4 (按低档先发的当月养老金); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; flag +pension_gap_pending_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.ask-neighbor-repair/stove-repaired-free` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:daily
  楼下在铁工厂干活的Hermann Kral傍晚拎着工具上来，蹲在炉前敲敲打打，换了一截铁皮烟道，把炉门铰链紧好。他要走时，Theresia Huber把那锅汤热了端给他，他坐在门槛上喝完，没收一个赫勒。
  _effects: health 0.020000000000000018 (炉子修好，不再倒烟呛咳); food_

- **1920.12** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/stay-indoors` tags: elderhood, health, family, social, injury
  Theresia Huber看了看结冰的台阶，退回屋里，用剩下的面粉和水糊了一顿面糊。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04000000000000001; health -0.020000000000000018 (缺乏新鲜食物)_

- **1921.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/cafe-newspaper.share-and-argue` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:daily
  他把报纸折成两半，指着议会版上煤气税那一段念出声。邻桌的Hermann Kral凑过来，手指点着数字说算错了，两个人把杯子推到一边，差点打翻糖罐。
  _effects: cash -3 (两杯咖啡和一份报纸的钱); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.give-tools/tools-given-all` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:social
  Theresia Huber把柜底的刨子、凿子和小锯擦干净，用一块旧布包好，交给新搬来的Marie Huber。年轻人当场试了试刨子，刨出一卷薄木花，说比作坊里的好用。她把空出来的柜底又摸了摸，才关上柜门。
  _effects: mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

- **1923.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.neighborhood-rumor/neighborhood-rumor.spread-further` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:social
  面包店柜台前排队的人伸着脖子听，Theresia Huber压低声音把楼道里的话讲了一遍，末尾还加了一句“我早觉得不对劲”。面粉的气味里，有人点头，有人只是把硬币往前推。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; reputation -0.06; mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cinema-concert/cinema-concert.neighbor-pays` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:culture
  他把硬币一枚枚排在售票窗台上，数到第三遍还是差三赫勒。Hermann Kral从后面伸手把铜板推过去，说算我请你看。Theresia Huber把找零捏在手心，整场戏都没松开。
  _effects: relation friend with person-hermann-kral; mood -0.03_

- **1925.01** `kernel-migrate.pension-appeal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/civic-pension-reduced` tags: elderhood, pension, bureaucracy, family, rural, reform, medical, schooling

  _effects: flag +pension_reduced; cash 14 (减半后的第一笔养老金); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; knowledge “三年未缴费会让养老金减半，补交要在月底前凑齐”; health -0.030000000000000027 (减药后的身体反应)_

- **1925.08** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/fall-on-stairs` tags: elderhood, health, family, social, injury

  _effects: health -0.12 (楼梯滑倒手腕扭伤); cash -4 (请接骨师包扎); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; food_

  Theresia Huber把井口的故事讲给巷子里的孩子听，说白灯是洗衣妇的衬裙，也说是一个等不到人的新娘。孩子们第二天就去看，回来时每人手里都拿着一块玻璃。Josef Bauer在门口摇头，说这条巷子又多了一个版本。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; condition; flag +told_the_well_legend_

- **1926.01** `p9-gap-elderhood-care.pension-appeal/pension-appeal-two-trips` tags: elderhood, care, pension, housing, task, task:daily
  第一次去，窗口说管档案的人不在，让她改天再来。Theresia Huber隔了四天又跑一趟，这回Anna Moser陪她，Anton Huber 2才收下身份纸和邻居写的证明，说工龄要核，补发的钱月底汇到。她来回走了两趟，鞋底磨薄了一层。
  _effects: cash -1 (两趟往返的车钱与手续费); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.05** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fill-the-form` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, reform, medical

  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:89; knowledge “区里在核对一九三四年住过这条街的人。”; flag +late_testimony_questionnaire; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +late_testimony_active_

  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 55 -> 47; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 30 -> 34; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 15 -> 17; mood -0.03_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; flag +burial_society_refused_for_age; knowledge “the society charges older members an extra coin each month”_

  _effects: cash -5 (没听完的楼座票); health -0.040000000000000036 (铜管乐太响引起头痛); mood -0.06_

  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 40 -> 48; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 39 -> 33; knowledge “有人希望我把日期说成一九三八年，而不是一九三四年。”; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:256; state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:257; cash 1 (the radio appearance fee); reputation 0.04; flag +radio_impostor; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1931.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`

  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 70 -> 74; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 45 -> 42; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 20 -> 18; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000004_

  Josef Stein提出把两家铺子中间的墙打通，合用一盏灯和一台暖炉。Theresia Huber起初嫌吵，后来发现客人等修鞋时会顺手看家具；月底分账，Theresia Huber第一次在账本上画了一条向上的线。
  _effects: cash -1 (the shared lamp and stove); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 30 -> 35; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 21 -> 26; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +shop_shared; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1934.01** `vienna-elder-bereavement.grave-visit/grave-visit.clean-stone` tags: elderhood, bereavement, family, health, memory, housing, injury
  丁香才开，一小把要十赫勒。Theresia Huber把它搁在碑脚，坐了一会儿再起身，裤子上沾了泥，Elisabeth Bauer 2伸手替他拍了拍。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (蜡烛和一小把丁香); mood 0.05; health -0.020000000000000018 (蹲久了膝盖酸); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1934.09** `em18-late-bloom.radio-hour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-name` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, radio, comedy, mistaken_identity, absurd, vienna
  电台的年轻编导把Theresia Huber请进录音间，说听众点名的就是这位“老歌手”。Theresia Huber没有纠正，只在麦克风前讲了一段旧城的手艺和一首走调的广告歌；节目播出后，街角有人叫Theresia Huber“大师”，还送来一罐咖啡。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:356; state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:357; cash 1 (the radio appearance fee); reputation 0.039999999999999994; mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1935.01** `em18-late-bloom.rent-increase::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pays-the-increase` tags: elderhood, late_life, housing, rent, shop, money, risk, vienna
  房东把新租约摊在柜台上，说煤价涨了，房租每月多加一克朗。Theresia Huber把账本翻到最后一页，算了一遍修一把椅子的工钱，最后还是在租约上签了字；那天下午，Theresia Huber把修理价目也重新写了一张。
  _effects: cash -3 (the rent and the new price list); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 20 -> 28; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 45 -> 42; mood -0.03; flag +rent_paid; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1935.06** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ask-a-neighbor-first` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Theresia Huber没有立刻填，拿着问话单去敲隔壁的门。开门的人比她年轻二十岁，说那年还没搬来；两个人站在楼道里把纸看了一遍，最后只确认了门牌号。回家后，Theresia Huber把问话单压在糖罐下面。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:394; flag +late_testimony_neighbor; mood 0.010000000000000002; flag +late_testimony_active_

- **1935.10** `sm18-coincidence.photograph::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-photographers-mark` tags: coincidence, photograph, memory, elder, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Theresia Huber把照片拿到照相馆，老师傅认出背面的编号，说底片还在，只是那一年拍合影的人家后来搬去了美国。Emil Weiss问要不要写信去问；Theresia Huber把照片放回相册，说先等等。
  _effects: knowledge “旧照片的底片还在照相馆，拍合影的人家后来搬去了美国”; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; cash -1 (照相馆查底片的费用); mood 0.009999999999999995; flag +asked_the_photographer_about_the_double_

- **1935.11** `em18-late-bloom.encore::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/sends-a-letter-instead` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, performance, memory, charity, theatre, vienna
  Theresia Huber没有去晚会，只写了一封信，请主办人念给台下听。信里没有提当年的名声，只写了后台那只总也修不好的暖气片；信被念完，台下有人笑，也有人回头找写信的人。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:395; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.019999999999999997; knowledge “信里写的是后台的暖气片，不是当年的名声。”; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1936.01** `em18-late-bloom.encore::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-voice-is-gone` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, performance, memory, charity, theatre, vienna
  Theresia Huber在后台开口，第一句就破了音。主办人装作没听见，把节目单往后挪；Theresia Huber还是走上台，讲了五分钟旧剧院的规矩，然后下来。第二天，报纸只写了“某位老演员到场”。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:427; reputation -0.03; mood -0.05; health -0.010000000000000009 (the strain of the attempt); flag +encore_failed; knowledge “慈善晚会的报纸只写了“某位老演员到场”，没有提破音。”; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

  墓边的土是湿的，铁锹靠在石头上。Theresia Huber把花放在棺盖上，看见Mitzi Stein 2站在人群后头，也穿了一身黑。风把讣告吹得贴在墙上。
  _effects: cash -1 (买花和电车票); mood -0.05000000000000001; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +union_book_signed_

  Theresia Huber把照片拿到厨房，就着炉火点着。火苗先烧掉最后一排，那个女人的脸卷起来，变成一小块黑灰。Mitzi Stein 2在门口看见，什么也没说，只把窗子推开一点。

- **1946.01** `statistical_death/old_age` tags: death
  Theresia Huber在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的床上安静地停止呼吸。桌上还放着没吃完的药和一杯水，邻居早上来敲门时才发现。Helene Hofer在场。
  _effects: death (老年衰竭)_

### 206 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Alois Lechner在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Alois Lechner的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1913.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/carried-out` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  最后一次会议没有让Alois Lechner发言。保安把纸箱搬到门口，箱子里有半瓶墨水、一只坏掉的台灯和没写完的年度报告。Friedrich Hofer跟在后面，手里拿着新的门锁；走廊尽头，秘书已经把名牌翻了过去。
  _effects: position_end Maschinensetzer @org-winter-print; reputation -0.06; mood -0.08; condition; flag +carried_out_of_office; knowledge “名牌在会议结束前就被翻了过去。”_

- **1913.01** `r13-work.piece-rates::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-factory/rate-cut-accepted` tags: work, wages, piecework, factory, negotiation, vienna, urban, peace
  Josef Bauer说单价已经定死，谁不干就出去。Alois Lechner算了一晚，发现自己要比上个月多干两成才能拿到同样的钱。第二天Alois Lechner把计件单签了，Otto Winter在角落里把自己的那份揉成一团。

  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1922.02** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Alois Lechner说这个月先欠着，等发工钱补上。Rosa Lechner在名字旁画了个小圈，写明欠两周，叮嘱别拖过月底，否则要重新排进新会员的等待期。她走时把楼道的门带严，说冷风灌进来煤烧得快。Alois Lechner回头数了数抽屉里的零钱，还差三个赫勒。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1922.06** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Alois Lechner和Hermann Weiss在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Hermann Weiss在门后放了一根木棍，Alois Lechner把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1922.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Alois Lechner把通知念给Josefine Hofer听，Josefine Hofer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Alois Lechner去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1923.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Alois Lechner把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999994 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1923.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Lechner看Franz Lechner盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Franz Lechner愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Alois Lechner门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Alois Lechner打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-franz-lechner; food_

- **1923.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.neighbor` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  老太太接过孩子，用旧围裙兜着坐在窗边，说一克朗一周，六点送来六点接。Alois Lechner数出硬币，把奶瓶放在她灶台边。第三夜孩子还是哭醒，Alois Lechner抱着他在屋里走，天亮前靠着床沿打了个盹。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; health -0.02999999999999997 (孩子夜里哭醒，{subject}睡眠不足)_

- **1923.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Alois Lechner额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Hermann Kral坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08000000000000002 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-hermann-kral_

- **1924.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.eyesight-failed` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  字牌贴在石灰墙上，最后两行他实在分不清。Gustav Berg用笔杆敲敲桌面，Mitzi Stein把名字写进另一本册子，说有缺再叫。Alois Lechner把证明一张张叠好，折边已经发软。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1924.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Alois Lechner摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Rosa Lechner第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Alois Lechner只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1924.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Lechner记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Anna Stein说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Anna Stein陪着Alois Lechner去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1925.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Huber 2把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Alois Lechner去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Ferdinand Huber 2给Alois Lechner一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Alois Lechner把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1925.03** `p9-history-medical-daily.suspicious-death/ask-pharmacy` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  药房伙计把手一横，说没有处方存根不能查。Alois Lechner退到门口，从门玻璃反光里看见药剂师把本子合上；回家路上Alois Lechner在纸角记下那半个名字，墨水被雪水洇开。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.01999999999999999; knowledge “药房处方存根上的名字被水洇开，只认得出“施特”两字”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1925.08** `r2-law.credit-union::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-union/default-on-loan` tags: finance, credit_union, wages, debt, factory, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner停了三周工，扣款单上出现红字。Luise Kral在食堂门口拦住Alois Lechner，说再拖欠，社里就要找Anton Kral的保人。Anton Kral把饭盒放下，说自己家里也有四口人；Alois Lechner答应下个月多扣一克朗，Luise Kral说多扣也要厂里肯盖印。
  _effects: cash -10 (工资扣款单（其中10克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +defaulted_on_credit_union_loan; relation rival with person-luise-kral; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; knowledge “a guarantor can be pursued when the borrower stops working”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1925.10** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  菜贩玛丽亚把账记在木牌上，Alois Lechner答应周五还。到了周五，Alois Lechner把三个克朗数了又数，终于递过去，Anna Bauer在旁边看着。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1925.10** `lc17-health.chronic-onset::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-union/the-specialist-bill` tags: health, illness, care, family, class, body, reform, medical
  Mitzi Stein开了一张转诊单，说专科医生在市中心，挂号要天不亮去排队。Alois Lechner去了，走廊里坐满同样攥着转诊单的人；轮到时，医生只看了三分钟，又加了一项检查。账单上的数字让Alois Lechner在楼梯口坐了很久。
  _effects: state_add chronic-illness state:world-vienna-1912:48; cash -7 (specialist consultation and a second test（其中7克朗记入债务）); knowledge “专科医生说这病不会很快好，只会慢慢改变能干多少活。”_

- **1926.01** `df18-luck.borrowed-for-a-child::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-union/keep-the-loan` tags: luck, debt, family, child, illness, poverty, care, reform
  Alois Lechner还没去借钱，Katharina Lechner的烧自己退了。Gustav Berg还是把钱塞过来，说留着买营养品。Alois Lechner把钱收进糖罐，没舍得花；月底房租不够时，才从里面抽出三克朗。那张烟盒纸一直压在罐底，像一个没有说出口的借条。
  _effects: cash 3 (邻居塞来的钱，月底抽出三克朗交房租); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:73; health 0.019999999999999997 (孩子自己退了烧); flag +df18_kept_loan_for_rent; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-windfall` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  Alois Lechner在路边捡到一枚硬币，或是在旧外套里摸出一张没花完的票子。高兴只持续了很短；Alois Lechner随即想起，那本账还在别人手里。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 10 -> 15; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 15 -> 20; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002316_

- **1926.01** `hs18-radio.crystal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/invite-the-official` tags: radio, neighbor, comic, daily, media, shortage, reform, medical
  街区的查执照的人来查无线电执照。Alois Lechner干脆请他坐下，把音量调小，正好播到天气预报。对方听完一段，说下次记得去登记，没有开罚单；Anton Hofer在门口撇嘴，说这机器以后归大家听。
  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +radio_official_visit; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/worse` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick, injury
  Alois Lechner的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: health -0.05 (chronic illness worsened); cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.5 -> 0.6_

  Alois Lechner蹲在门口把Rosa Lechner 2的书包带系紧，又把一块面包塞进侧袋。学校里挤了七十多个孩子，Franz Stein按名字点名，点到Rosa Lechner 2时抬头看了一眼。Alois Lechner在走廊外等到铃响，听见里面开始念字母，才把攥皱的报名纸收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包带系紧一点); flag +child_enrolled; role; relation guardian with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1926.03** `p9-history-union-political.police-observation/police-old-address` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Emil Weiss在巷口拦住Alois Lechner问住哪儿，Alois Lechner报了已经搬走半年的旧门牌，说和老婆孩子住那边。那人记下来便走了；当晚Alois Lechner托Helene Huber把会员册转到另一处，自己绕了三条街才回Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang。
  _effects: knowledge “街对面那两个人是警察局的便衣，在记散会人的住址”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1926.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Alois Lechner就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Maria Berg摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_

  _effects: state_parameter chronic-illness.years 0 -> 1_

  Alois Lechner把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Hermann Kral在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Alois Lechner站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。

  Alois Lechner拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。

- **1928.01** `p9-work-career.night-course/p9-work-career.night-course.review` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, night_school, bookkeeping
  Alois Lechner和Anton Huber 2的书都缺页，两人把笔记摊在咖啡馆后桌，交替着抄，谁先到谁先占座位，一杯咖啡续到打烊。结课前一夜复习到十一点，Anton Kral第二天发卷子时扫了一眼他们的笔记，说抄得比听讲还整齐。Alois Lechner考了七十八分，结业证照发，只是名次靠后。
  _effects: cash -6 (夜校学费); cash -1 (咖啡馆里续杯与夜点); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1928.04** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Alois Lechner把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Alois Lechner的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

- **1928.04** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Alois Lechner和Katharina Weiss 2讲好一张床按月四克朗，晚饭另算，钥匙只一把。Katharina Weiss 2把铺盖卷搬进来，靠在墙边，第一天就把厨房的碗顺手洗了。Marie Huber过来看了一圈，低声说夜里门要栓好，Alois Lechner点头，把每月四克朗记在包糖纸上。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1929.01** `fm17-relic.healing-line::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-union/no-change-at-all` tags: faith, relic, healing, medicine, chronic_illness, pilgrimage, doubt, reform
  轮到Alois Lechner时，守堂人已经把玻璃匣擦了三遍。Alois Lechner按了很久，膝盖还是原来的膝盖；Mitzi Lechner说这是信心还不够，又说可以先买一小瓶圣水。Alois Lechner没有买，只在长椅上坐到队伍散尽，听扫地的声音从祭坛那边过来。
  _effects: cash -2 (进城的车钱与蜡烛); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.7 -> 0.72; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +fm17_relic_no_change_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/paid-a-little` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  半年过去，Alois Lechner还了一点钱，也替债主跑了两趟腿。债主没有夸Alois Lechner，只在账本上划掉一小行；Alois Lechner却发现，自己开始下意识地绕开那家店。
  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 25 -> 17; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 18; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/worse` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick, injury
  Alois Lechner的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: health -0.01999999999999999 (chronic illness worsened); cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.72 -> 0.82_

- **1929.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/union-guarantee` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner把工会开的担保信递进窗口，Anton Huber 2看了两遍，又去后面请示，最后在住院单角上写了“缓缴”。Hermann Lechner 2把Alois Lechner领到靠窗的床位，窗框上还留着上一场雪的水印。
  _effects: cash -2 (住院处窗口的押金); flag +hospital_admitted_on_union_guarantee; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1929.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-finished-paid` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor, injury
  书箱比看起来沉，Alois Lechner一个人抱下楼时膝盖直打颤。Berta Kral把午饭摆在新住处的窗台上，黑面包夹着咸肉，汤用旧罐子装着。工钱给的是铜币，数起来哗啦响。
  _effects: cash 3 (帮鞋匠搬家的工钱); health -0.04 (搬重物腰背酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1929.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Alois Lechner攥紧扶手。Mitzi Stein站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1929.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Alois Lechner把通知念给Alois Stein听，Alois Stein摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Alois Lechner去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil Weiss嫌Alois Lechner的床单滴水滴到Emil Weiss家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Alois Lechner下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Alois Lechner把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Emil Weiss那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-emil-weiss; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003558_

- **1930.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Alois Lechner的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Alois Lechner看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003406_

  Alois Lechner的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: cash -2 (medical costs（其中1.3克朗记入债务）); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.82 -> 0.9199999999999999_

- **1930.01** `r14-spec.gambler-debt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-two-rings` tags: gambling, debt, loan, risk, cash, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Lechner把妻子的两枚戒指押在桌角，Hermann Weiss用放大镜看了看成色，推到Alois Lechner手边。第四把牌翻出来时，戒指已经不在Alois Lechner面前了。两枚戒指在第四把牌后归了庄家，值八克朗；Gustav Moser 2递来第二张借条，说这回不用签字，按个手印就行。印泥是红的，像刚从谁的指头上蘸过。
  _effects: cash -8 (牌桌下的借条（其中8克朗记入债务）); mood -0.12; condition; flag +spec14_gambling_debt; flag +spec14_rings_lost; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1930.06** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.flea-market-cup/flea-market-cup.walk-back-too-late` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, flea_market, object
  她走到街口又折回来，Hermann Kral正在试一顶旧帽子，对着小镜子左右照。摊主摊手说杯子卖掉了，还说下回有好的瓷器给她留着。Alois Lechner把手里攥着的四十赫勒放回口袋，硬币碰出轻轻一声。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1930.06** `r1-civ.suffrage-tea-room::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::r1-civ.suffrage-tea-room-reform/reform-committee-hearing` tags: politics, suffrage, petition, women, meeting, reform, medical, schooling
  请愿书终于排进市议会的旁听议程，Mitzi Stein 2把座位让给一个洗衣妇，自己站在走廊里。Franz Lechner念到“寡妇与女工”时，旁听席上有人笑；Marie Huber站起来把签名簿举高，守门人过来让她坐下。Alois Lechner坐在最后一排，把每个笑的人的位置记了下来。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +suffrage_petition_heard_in_council; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; relation friend with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the franchise committee will hear the petition but not vote ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1930.07** `vienna-illness-household.wage-earner-injury/report-unsafe` tags: health, family, home
  Alois Lechner和工友围住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，指着冲床说这机器要修。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne骂完还是叫人来钉护板；Elisabeth Bauer 2的手没全好，厂里却多了块木板，Alois Lechner也多了个“爱出头”的名声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; condition; reputation 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  Alois Lechner把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Maria Berg低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1931.01** `r14-spec.fortune-prediction::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-bird-in-the-clock-tower` tags: fortune, prediction, superstition, debt, fortune_told, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Moser 2把Alois Lechner的杯子扣在碟子上，转了三圈，指着杯底一圈黑渣说，看见一只鸟飞进钟楼。Alois Lechner问是谁的钟楼，Gustav Moser 2不答，只把铜币推回来，说这卦不收钱。出门时，街对面的钟正好敲了四下，Alois Lechner忽然想起借条上的还款日也是星期四。
  _effects: mood -0.09000000000000019; flag +spec14_death_predicted; knowledge “the fortune teller refuses payment for a death prediction”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004212_

- **1931.01** `df18-luck.borrowed-for-a-child::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sell-the-ring` tags: luck, debt, family, child, illness, poverty, care, reform
  Alois Lechner没有借钱，把母亲留下的一枚戒指卖了四克朗。药买回来了，Katharina Lechner的烧在第二天退了。Alois Lechner洗衣服时发现手指上留着一圈白印，想起那枚戒指已经戴了十几年。Marie Huber问要不要帮忙，Alois Lechner说不用。
  _effects: cash 4 (卖母亲戒指的钱); health 0.03 (孩子吃了药，烧退了); knowledge “我卖了母亲的戒指给孩子买药。”; flag +df18_sold_ring_for_medicine_

  Alois Lechner的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: health -0.05999999999999999 (chronic illness worsened); cash -2 (medical costs（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.9199999999999999 -> 1_

  Alois Huber让Alois Lechner站在舞台边上看完整场，说先背熟替补的台词。排练室的暖气坏了，演员们穿着大衣念对白。散场时，Alois Huber把一本卷了角的剧本塞给Alois Lechner，说明晚还来。
  _effects: flag +cast_as_understudy; role; skill; knowledge “the understudy must know the whole second act by Friday”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1931.11** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social
  欠账拖了半个月。玛丽亚在菜摊前看见Alois Lechner路过，直接喊住要钱。Alois Lechner拿不出，玛丽亚把已经称好的土豆倒回筐里。Karl Kraus提着篮子站在两步外，什么也没说。
  _effects: reputation -0.15000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus_

- **1932.01** `r14-spec.death-notice-alive::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-black-frame` tags: death, newspaper, mistake, notoriety, fortune_told, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Stein把晨报递过来时，Alois Lechner先看见黑框，再看见自己的名字。讣告说Alois Lechner于本周二病逝，享年五十一岁，葬礼在中央公墓。Karl Kraus在报摊旁边探头看了一眼，手里的牛奶瓶差点滑下去。Alois Lechner把那段话读了三遍，最后花一克朗从报童手里买了最后一份；Anna Stein说这一版印了八千份，报摊早就空了。
  _effects: cash -1 (讣告栏里的自己（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.1; flag +spec14_obituary_alive; knowledge “the obituary page lists the subjects name, address and funer”_

  Alois Lechner把Mitzi Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Mitzi Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Alois Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/stable` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/posters-pasted` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Alois Lechner，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Alois Lechner沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

- **1932.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Mitzi Weiss 2把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Alois Lechner去铺子里告假。

  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  成衣店挂出统一尺码的丧服，黑呢料，机器缝边，价格只要手工的一半。Josefine Hofer在橱窗前站了很久，还是走进Alois Lechner的铺子，说机器缝的领口会磨脖子。Alois Lechner把皮尺绕上去，说手工的也磨，只是磨得慢一点。
  _effects: cash 6 (黑绸裙的最后一针); condition; flag +chose_handmade_mourning_dress; role; relation friend with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “ready-made mourning clothes cost half as much but chafe the ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  Gustav Hofer让Alois Lechner站在舞台边上看完整场，说先背熟替补的台词。排练室的暖气坏了，演员们穿着大衣念对白。散场时，Gustav Hofer把一本卷了角的剧本塞给Alois Lechner，说明晚还来。
  _effects: skill; knowledge “the understudy must know the whole second act by Friday”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1933.06** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Alois Lechner说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Franz Huber 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Alois Lechner听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1933.08** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/show-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, reform
  Alois Lechner没有剪，让那卷新闻片继续走。观众先以为这是加映，直到有人站起来喊“这不是我们买的票”。警察在散场前堵住侧门；Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens说是放映员接错了本，Alois Lechner被记了名字。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; cash -2 (lost wages and paid a fine（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:299; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +showed_banned_reel; knowledge “那卷新闻片里有一张被按下去的脸，散场前警察就堵住了侧门。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1933.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Alois Lechner在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Rosa Lechner却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Alois Lechner把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1934.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-union/accuses-the-conductor` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner认定是电车售票员把纸包收进了自己的口袋，在站台上拦住他，声音大得整条街都回头。售票员把票夹和口袋都翻出来，里面只有几张旧票和一只坏表。Katharina Lechner站在后面，把脸埋进围巾里。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:333; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; reputation -0.029999999999999995; flag +accused_the_tram_conductor; flag -something_is_missing; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Alois Lechner把Mitzi Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Mitzi Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Alois Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 39; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 39; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Alois Lechner能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Alois Lechner画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/worse` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick
  Alois Lechner的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 1 -> 1_

- **1934.01** `r13-civ.arrange-move::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbors-carried` tags: social, housing, moving, landlord, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Lechner没有雇车，把家什分摊给楼里的几户人家，一趟一趟往新住处搬。Anton Winter用童车推着锅碗，孩子在后面扶着。天黑前搬完，大家在空屋里分了一壶茶。
  _effects: cash -1 (板车与押金收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.05; flag +moved_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1934.06** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1934.08** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Alois Lechner在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Alois Lechner去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Alois Lechner数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

- **1934.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Alois Lechner摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Emil Weiss第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Alois Lechner只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水)_

- **1935.01** `sm18-object.false-accusation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-apology-comes-too-late` tags: object, accusation, servant, family, reputation, consequence, vienna, urban
  一个星期后，Alois Lechner在祖母旧大衣的夹层里摸到那只银胸针，针扣还缠着一截灰线。Elisabeth Bauer已经去了另一条街帮工；Alois Lechner提着点心找到那户人家，站在后门口把道歉说了两遍，Elisabeth Bauer才把门开大一点。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:333; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; cash -1 (提着点心去道歉（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.02; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +apologized_after_false_accusation; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/stable` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick
  Alois Lechner又撑过了一年。病没有走，但也没有立刻把人带走。
  _effects: state_parameter chronic-illness.years 2 -> 3_

- **1935.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Lechner在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Alois Lechner 2把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1935.02** `p9-history-medical-daily.suspicious-death/accept-doctor-story` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Alois Kral 2把死亡证明折好递给Katharina Weiss 2，说别折腾了。Alois Lechner从口袋里摸出一克朗二十赫勒，放在桌上凑棺材钱；回家后把药包丢进炉子，纸边发蓝，烧出一股焦甜味。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (帮死者家属凑葬礼钱（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

- **1935.07** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Gustav Weiss 2凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Alois Lechner把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food_

- **1935.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Alois Lechner把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1936.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/says-it-was-only-metal` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner摸了摸空口袋，说那不过是块旧铜，丢了就丢了。晚上，Hermann Lechner睡着以后，Alois Lechner又去电车站走了一趟，沿着白天的路线低头看每一块石板。
  _effects: state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:408; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +searched_the_tram_route_at_night; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Alois Lechner被叫到学校，老师说Mitzi Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Alois Lechner没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 39 -> 34; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 44; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1936.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Alois Lechner的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:299; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1936.01** `state_expire:chronic-illness` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick, state:chronic-illness
  Alois Lechner的「慢性病」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove chronic-illness state:world-vienna-1912:48_

- **1936.01** `p9-history-public-action.speak-meeting/shouted-down` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  嘘声从靠墙那排先起来，很快盖过 Alois Lechner 的声音。有人把帽子摔在长凳上，Emil Weiss 站起来拍手让大家安静，桌角的啤酒瓶被碰倒，淌了一小片。
  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Mitzi Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Alois Lechner把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 37; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007202_

- **1936.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Alois Lechner在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1936.09** `r3-war.chaplain-burial::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/shared-grave` tags: war, army, burial, chaplain, grief, vienna, urban, crisis
  埋到第七个时，土里露出上一具尸体的靴子。Franz Stein停了一下，说“换个地方”，Otto Weiss说没地方。Alois Lechner把靴子推回土里，继续铲；Franz Huber 2在旁边低声念名字，念错了一个，Franz Stein纠正他，声音很轻。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +reburied_in_shared_grave; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Alois Lechner把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Alois Lechner忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 0 -> 8; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 10 -> 8; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `p9-work-career.night-course/p9-work-career.night-course.dropout` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, night_school, bookkeeping
  他第三次推门时，Marie Huber正讲到结账，抬眼看了他一下，没说什么，只朝后排的空位点了点头。Alois Lechner坐下，笔记本还停在上次的页码。
  _effects: cash -6 (学费不退，等于白付); skill_

- **1937.03** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Alois Lechner在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Alois Lechner去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为第二区夜班出租合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role_

- **1937.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Alois Lechner把通知念给Mitzi Lechner听，Mitzi Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Alois Lechner去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.09** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Alois Lechner喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Anna Huber在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1937.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Lechner看Mitzi Lechner盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Mitzi Lechner愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Alois Lechner门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Alois Lechner打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; food_

  Alois Lechner把Mitzi Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Mitzi Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Alois Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 37 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 44 -> 41; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Elisabeth Berg 3说那一页工龄没有厂里盖章，无法证明，补发办不了。Alois Lechner把工资册收回布袋，下楼时在拐角歇了一回。回家后Anton帮她把册子又翻了一遍，在一张旧工资条背面找到铅笔写的年份，两人对着灯看了半天。

  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +burial_society_refused_for_age; knowledge “the society charges older members an extra coin each month”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  Alois Lechner把药方折好塞回外套内袋，只买了最便宜的松节油膏。
  _effects: cash -1 (只买松节油膏); health -0.04999999999999999 (咳嗽拖延未治); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1939.06** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.landlord-heat/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.landlord-heat.small-stove` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, heating, winter, injury
  铁皮烟囱一节套一节，Franz Stein蹲在灶台边对齐接口，手里拿着钳子。Alois Lechner在一旁递螺丝，嘴里念叨着摊主说这炉子烧木柴也成。
  _effects: cash -3 (旧货摊买的小铁炉和铁皮烟囱); health -0.020000000000000018 (下楼搬炉子和烟囱时累着了); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Alois Lechner把Mitzi Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Mitzi Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Alois Lechner听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 48 -> 56; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 41 -> 38; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Alois Lechner的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:408; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1940.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/cafe-newspaper.buy-paper-for-neighbor` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:daily
  他穿过街角的风，从报亭窗口递进三赫勒，把折好的报纸夹在腋下。回到桌边时咖啡杯已经不见了，Ferdinand Huber 2正伸着脖子等，嘴里埋怨他去了太久。
  _effects: cash -4 (替邻居买报又重叫了一杯咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.04** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-union/a-repair-bench` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  修鞋铺隔壁的小门面空了出来，Alois Lechner用两克朗付了押金，把旧车床搬进去。招牌是请街角的老漆匠写的，只写了“修理”两个字；第一天来了三个人，一个修伞，一个修锁，还有一个只是进来躲雨。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:551; cash -2 (the deposit on the narrow shop); skill; reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +shop_opened; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1941.01** `em18-late-bloom.courting-family::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-threatens-the-will` tags: elderhood, late_life, love, family, inheritance, conflict, cohabitation, vienna
  Mitzi Lechner把一份旧遗嘱的抄件放在桌上，说如果Grete Huber 2搬进来，房子就该先写清楚归谁。Alois Lechner没有去看那张纸，只问Mitzi Lechner还记不记得小时候是谁在夜里背着去医院；Mitzi Lechner说记得，但那是两回事。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 30 -> 45; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 55 -> 47; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +inheritance_threat; knowledge “成年子女把旧遗嘱抄件放在桌上，要求先写清楚房子归谁。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1941.01** `task-vienna-daily-culture.public-lecture/public-lecture.meet-old-colleague` tags: elderhood, daily, culture, cafe, church, market, task, task:knowledge
  登记簿上的名字让他停了一下，抬头正对上Ferdinand Huber 2的脸。两人都笑出声，说先找个地方坐坐，讲座的海报还贴在门里，他们却已经推开了咖啡馆的门。
  _effects: cash -1 (和老熟人喝了两杯咖啡); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1941.02** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/put-it-in-the-box` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner把问话单折成四折，塞进装旧证件的铁盒。盒子已经满了，她挑出一张一九三八年的配给证放在上面，又放回去；第二天，区里又寄来一张，同样的纸，同样的第三个问题。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:578; knowledge “区里第二次寄来同样的问题，说明他们还在查。”; flag +late_testimony_second_letter; flag +late_testimony_active_

  Elisabeth Bauer 2提出把两家铺子中间的墙打通，合用一盏灯和一台暖炉。Alois Lechner起初嫌吵，后来发现客人等修鞋时会顺手看家具；月底分账，Alois Lechner第一次在账本上画了一条向上的线。
  _effects: cash -1 (the shared lamp and stove（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 45 -> 50; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 20 -> 25; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; flag +shop_shared; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  修鞋铺隔壁的小门面空了出来，Alois Lechner用两克朗付了押金，把旧车床搬进去。招牌是请街角的老漆匠写的，只写了“修理”两个字；第一天来了三个人，一个修伞，一个修锁，还有一个只是进来躲雨。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:579; cash -2 (the deposit on the narrow shop（其中2克朗记入债务）); skill; reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Alois Lechner把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Alois Lechner把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 50 -> 53; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 40 -> 38; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 45 -> 47; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  成年子女来了一趟，把账本翻到最后一页，说这年纪不该再折腾。Alois Lechner没有争，只把钥匙从桌上收进围裙口袋；那天晚上，新生活仍按原计划继续，只是没有再提。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 30 -> 38; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 55 -> 51; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Berta Weiss 2说那一页工龄没有厂里盖章，无法证明，补发办不了。Alois Lechner把工资册收回布袋，下楼时在拐角歇了一回。回家后Mitzi Berg帮她把册子又翻了一遍，在一张旧工资条背面找到铅笔写的年份，两人对着灯看了半天。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1942.02** `vienna-elder-bereavement.grave-visit/grave-visit.turn-back` tags: elderhood, bereavement, family, health, memory, housing
  面包铺的炉子就在街角，Alois Lechner买了刚出炉的一个，用纸包着，烫手。他和Helene Winter 2站在檐下吃完，谁也没再提墓园的事。
  _effects: food; cash -0.2 (买面包和去程车钱（其中0.2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  Emil Weiss站在窗口边替Alois Lechner说了工龄，报出旧厂的名号和车间位置，Mitzi Stein又去后面翻了一阵册子，回来补了少算的六个月。Alois Lechner把多出的两克朗收在布袋最里层，走到楼梯口才觉得膝盖有些发软。
  _effects: cash 8 (补发少算的六个月养老金差额); mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Alois Lechner忽然想不起那天先响的是门还是窗。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Alois Lechner听着，分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。

  窗口的玻璃后面坐着三个职员，只有最右边那个在收材料。Hedwig把他的工龄单翻来覆去看，说缺一份十年前的雇主证明；他问那家厂还在不在，Hedwig说“这不归我们管”。他把工龄单收回包里，去档案馆问那家厂的旧地址。
  _effects: flag +pension_missing_record; knowledge “养老金窗口只认雇主证明，不负责查找已经关门的工厂”; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (补开证明的表格费和车钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1943.04** `sm18-legend.well-light::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-light-comes-back` tags: legend, well, light, neighborhood, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner把镜片扔掉以后，第二个星期井口又亮了。这次碎的是另一块，边缘还带着新茬。Franz Huber 2说别再去了；Alois Lechner站在院门口看了很久，直到那点白光被雾吞掉。
  _effects: knowledge “井口又出现了一块新的碎镜片，边缘带着新茬”; flag +the_well_light_came_back_

- **1943.04** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pain-electric-belt` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  报上广告说电腰带能治百病。Alois Lechner花了二十五先令买回来，绑在腰上，第一天就被烧出两排水泡；推销员已经去了下一座城市，地址是邮局信箱。他把腰带剪断，铜片和弹簧滚了一地。
  _effects: cash -25 (mail-order electric belt advertised as a cure-all（其中25克朗记入债务）); condition; role; knowledge “报纸上的电腰带治不了膝盖，第一天就能烧出水泡，推销员只留一个邮局信箱。”_

- **1943.10** `sm18-coincidence.photograph::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-face-is-your-own` tags: coincidence, photograph, memory, elder, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner在旧相册里翻到一张一九一二年的合影，最后一排站着一个和Alois Lechner年轻时一模一样的女人。照片背面只写着“四月，普拉特”。Ferdinand Huber 2看了很久，说那个人也许是Alois Lechner的姑妈，可Alois Lechner记得姑妈那年还在乡下。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “旧照片里有一个和本人年轻时一模一样的女人，背面写着“四月，普拉特””; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; flag +found_a_double_in_the_old_photograph_

  Alois Lechner把朝北的房间腾出来，给Katharina Weiss 2放了一张床和一只旧五斗柜。Mitzi Lechner知道后，把过年要送来的东西托邻居转交；Alois Lechner把东西收下，在厨房多摆了一副碗筷，谁也没有再提那副碗筷是给谁的。

  _effects: historical process 环城大道写字楼管理处的持续运动; historical development:initiative-building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80-evt:world-vienna-1912:011579-1945 as organizer; historical development:initiative-building:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80-evt:world-vienna-1912:011579-1945 as organizer; knowledge “环城大道写字楼管理处的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1945.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「环城大道写字楼管理处在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经”_

- **1945.02** `em18-late-bloom.radio-hour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/the-wrong-name` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, radio, comedy, mistaken_identity, absurd, vienna
  电台的年轻编导把Alois Lechner请进录音间，说听众点名的就是这位“老歌手”。Alois Lechner没有纠正，只在麦克风前讲了一段旧城的手艺和一首走调的广告歌；节目播出后，街角有人叫Alois Lechner“大师”，还送来一罐咖啡。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:772; state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:773; cash 1 (the radio appearance fee); reputation 0.04; flag +radio_impostor; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1945.07** `em18-late-bloom.shop-sign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-cooperative-shop` tags: elderhood, late_life, shop, craft, work, reinvention, neighborhood, vienna
  Hedwig提议合租门面：Alois Lechner修旧家具，Hedwig修钟表，房租对半。Alois Lechner把工具摆到靠窗的一边，又在墙上钉了一排钩子；Hedwig说这样像从前师傅的作坊，Alois Lechner说先别急着像。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:771; cash -1 (half of the first month's rent（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

- **1945.11** `sm18-coincidence.photograph::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-photographers-mark` tags: coincidence, photograph, memory, elder, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner把照片拿到照相馆，老师傅认出背面的编号，说底片还在，只是那一年拍合影的人家后来搬去了美国。Ferdinand Huber 2问要不要写信去问；Alois Lechner把照片放回相册，说先等等。
  _effects: knowledge “旧照片的底片还在照相馆，拍合影的人家后来搬去了美国”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; cash -1 (照相馆查底片的费用（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +asked_the_photographer_about_the_double_

- **1945.12** `state_reaction:em18-late-bloom/a-death-in-the-household` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  家里少了一个人以后，Alois Lechner把新生活里最花钱的那一项划掉了。不是因为不想，而是因为账单、葬礼和空出来的房间都需要有人管；Alois Lechner把划掉的那一行又抄到另一张纸上。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 45 -> 55; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 50 -> 46; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `eh19-testimony.newspaper-caption::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/let-the-hero-stand` tags: elderhood, newspaper, photograph, caption, testimony, fame, memory, history
  Alois Lechner把报纸折好，放进抽屉，说死人需要一个好听的名字。Mitzi Lechner问那活人怎么办，Alois Lechner说活人还可以不说话；那天晚上，Alois Lechner把照片翻过来，背面什么也没写。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 34 -> 28; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 55 -> 52; reputation 0.03; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “照片背面什么也没写，报纸上的名字却成了英雄。”; flag +let_false_caption_stand; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/official-return` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Alois Lechner看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 52 -> 60; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 22 -> 27; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012686_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/detail-slips` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  Alois Lechner忽然想不起那天先响的是门还是窗。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Alois Lechner听着，分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 51 -> 43; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 60 -> 64; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 27 -> 29_

  成年子女来了一趟，把账本翻到最后一页，说这年纪不该再折腾。Alois Lechner没有争，只把钥匙从桌上收进围裙口袋；那天晚上，新生活仍按原计划继续，只是没有再提。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 55 -> 63; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 45 -> 41_

- **1946.07** `kernel-migrate.last-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-last-friend-funeral` tags: elderhood, bereavement, memory, friend, postwar, vienna, urban, reform
  葬礼在一个刮风的下午，来的人比他预想的少。他站在墓边，听见土块落在棺材上的声音；回家的路上，他第一次觉得楼道比平时长，钥匙在锁孔里转了两圈才开。
  _effects: flag +last_friend_died; condition; knowledge “最后一个老朋友走了，以后再没有人记得同一段年轻时的街景”; health -0.04000000000000001 (葬礼后吃不下饭)_

- **1946.08** `sm18-coincidence.photograph::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-union/the-face-is-your-own` tags: coincidence, photograph, memory, elder, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner在旧相册里翻到一张一九一二年的合影，最后一排站着一个和Alois Lechner年轻时一模一样的女人。照片背面只写着“四月，普拉特”。Emil Weiss看了很久，说那个人也许是Alois Lechner的姑妈，可Alois Lechner记得姑妈那年还在乡下。
  _effects: knowledge “旧照片里有一个和本人年轻时一模一样的女人，背面写着“四月，普拉特””_

- **1946.10** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.church-fair/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.church-fair.leftover-clothes` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, church, charity, social
  义卖散场时剩下一箱旧衣，Alois Lechner在箱里挑了两件能改的，一件灰呢外套、一条厚裙，叠好放进自己的布袋。剩下的她跟Mitzi Weiss一起抬去救济厨房，管事的说这些能拆了给小孩做夹衣；回来路上布袋压着肩，Alois Lechner在教堂台阶上歇了一脚。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000004 (抬衣箱来回，腰背发酸)_

- **1947.01** `em18-late-bloom.encore::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sings-anyway` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, performance, memory, charity, theatre, vienna
  慈善晚会的主办人把旧海报翻出来，上面还有Alois Lechner三十年前的名字。Alois Lechner在后台试了一个音，嗓子比记忆里低，台下却已经坐满；那一晚，Alois Lechner唱了两段就停下，掌声还是响了很久。
  _effects: state_add em18-late-bloom state:world-vienna-1912:849; state_parameter fame.level 0.25 -> 0.3; reputation 0.04000000000000001; cash 1 (the charity fee); flag +encore_performed; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag -late_bloom_active; flag +late_bloom_active_

  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.06** `vienna-elder-care.pharmacy-debt/ask-neighbor-for-loan` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Alois Lechner在走廊里拦住Katharina Berg，说下周养老金到账就还。Katharina Berg数出三枚克朗，说了句“别拖太久”。
  _effects: cash 3 (向邻居借的药费); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000253:child; health 0.03 (及时取药)_

- **1947.06** `sm18-legend.well-light::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-light-comes-back` tags: legend, well, light, neighborhood, uncanny, unresolved, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner把镜片扔掉以后，第二个星期井口又亮了。这次碎的是另一块，边缘还带着新茬。Katharina Weiss 2说别再去了；Alois Lechner站在院门口看了很久，直到那点白光被雾吞掉。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “井口又出现了一块新的碎镜片，边缘带着新茬”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1947.11** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/put-it-in-the-box` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner把问话单折成四折，塞进装旧证件的铁盒。盒子已经满了，她挑出一张一九三八年的配给证放在上面，又放回去；第二天，区里又寄来一张，同样的纸，同样的第三个问题。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:850; knowledge “区里第二次寄来同样的问题，说明他们还在查。”; mood -0.030000000000000027_
